### ✓ 2026-10-04: Write article on self-control as skill and scaffolding — what the strategy and skill literature shows, what it relocates for a conscious selector, and what it costs the effort-as-evidence argument
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/self-control-as-skill-and-scaffolding.md
- **Notes**: Write from the research note at /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/research/self-control-as-skill-and-scaffolding-2026-10-04.md; read it IN FULL first, especially §Verdict, §Key Sources B–G, §What This Does to an Effortful Controller (RQ2), §Map Relevance and §Corpus Seams. TITLE "Self-Control as Skill and Scaffolding"; slug self-control-as-skill-and-scaffolding (free in obsidian/, archive/, hugo/content/ on 2026-10-04); section concepts, 346/360 on 2026-10-04 (re-measure with tools.evolution.state.count_section_files). `topics:` must be bare slugs and non-empty (e.g. akrasia-and-weakness-of-will, the-divided-will, philosophy-of-habit-under-dualism). TIER VERDICT, in natural prose with no bracketed tier labels: the trait pattern (high trait self-control tracks automatic behaviour, habits and fewer effortful-restraint events) is strongly supported; "scaffolding does most of the work" is a live hypothesis with verified counterweights (Milyavskaya, Saunders & Inzlicht 2021: inhibition similarly effective; Hennecke et al. 2019: strategies do not explain the trait's benefits); "effortful resistance is causally idle" is not established (one study, Milyavskaya & Inzlicht 2017). The Map reading is RELOCATION: synchronic conscious control as developmental foundation and emergency reserve (Bermúdez 2021: "explanatory primacy" vs "practical prevalence"; "an emergency measure"), with ordinary success at policy moments, matching skill-delegation's calibration/withdrawal/override shape. That is framework-internal coherence ONLY: a physical planner fits equally, and under the decorative-falsifier rule the page may neither list "self-control is mostly scaffolding" as a challenge nor count the fit as support (P-M1; stay consistent with control-theoretic-will L130, "no current prediction separates a non-physical controller from a physical one"). State the one cost: felt effort marks where control is hard, not necessarily where conscious contribution is largest; every skill model reads felt effort as a cost signal (Kurzban), which mental-effort L88 already files on the materialist side. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS: (1) Bermúdez 2021 Synthese 199:6251–6273 is JUAN PABLO Bermúdez, not José Luis. (2) Irving, Bridges, Glasser, Bermúdez & Sripada 2022 Cognition 225:105154 concludes "synchronic regulation is the sole difference maker in the folk concept of self-control": cite it as folk-concept evidence on the SYNCHRONIC side, never as support for scaffolding; the Gopnik/Pinter "mind-wandering" paper (Cognitive Science 2020) is a different paper, do not cite it. (3) Sripada is a synchronic-skill theorist; his denial that diachronic strategies count as self-control is [2nd] via Bermúdez n. 19, so attribute it as Bermúdez's report. (4) Kennett 2001 DEFENDS the possibility of synchronic self-control; diachronic control is "relatively unproblematic" (p. 135). (5) SEP §3.4.2 (Stroud & Svirsky 2025) says scaffolding "may be more effective": do not upgrade it, and do not reproduce the outer review's "do most of the work". (6) Mylopoulos & Pacherie pages are 81–100; the SEP's 81–115 is wrong. (7) Hofmann et al. 2012's fewer-temptations finding is NOT in its abstract: cite it only as Bermúdez reads it, or omit it. (8) Mischel & Baker 1975 and Kennett & Smith 1996 are metadata only: make no content claims. (9) Levy 2011 is a depletion account: do not cite Levy as a scaffolding theorist. ACCESS LEVELS: quote [F] spans (SEP; Bermúdez 2021) and [S] spans (Kennett; Ainslie, Breakdown of Will, with the page numbers given) only exactly as the note gives them; use [A] items only as abstract wording, attributed as such; nothing from [M] or [Lead] items. GUARDS: no view on the Tenet 3 quantifier (actual vs capacity; referred to the operator); no universal claim that consciousness is causally efficacious; leave the bi-aspectual tension (aspects vs persisting subject) open; do NOT present scaffolding as a reply to the luck objection (it only moves the contrastive question to the moment of resolution); never describe the selector as a reservoir of strength; keep folk-concept data apart from efficacy data. SIBLING COORDINATION with topics/the-divided-will (written and deep-reviewed 2026-10-04): that page owns Ainslie's personal rules as the way a will emerges without an organ, plus the organ-model test and the luck objection. THIS page owns personal rules as a STRATEGY: the four tactics (Breakdown pp. 74–89), the 2021 resolve/suppression split, habit as "a successful result of resolve", the rigidity costs (pp. 148, 152; Kennett p. 149) and hot/cool cool coding at abstract level. Link to the organ test and the luck objection rather than restating them, and do not contradict the sibling's verdict (fragmentation is predicted by partitioned/distributed architectures and only accommodated by a unitary selector). LENGTH: concepts soft 2,500 / hard 3,500 (analyze_length counts references; gate >=); target ≤2,900 total. INTEGRATION (headroom = hard − 1 − count, measured 2026-10-04; re-measure): skill-delegation L56, one sentence ≤45 words + Further Reading (2,383/3,500, headroom 1,116); control-theoretic-will L148 "Adaptive control", one sentence ≤40 words + Further Reading (2,649, 850); mental-effort L72, scope "only selection explains why choosing feels effortful" to the Map's reading (about +4) + Further Reading (3,310, 189; total ≤ +25); akrasia-and-weakness-of-will L82, zero-word pipe on "a set of devices that compensate" + Further Reading (3,598/4,000, 401); the-divided-will, Further Reading only (3,055/4,000, 944); phenomenology-of-choice-and-volition, Further Reading link ONLY, because L3/L94/L123 are held by the open NEEDS-HUMAN entry on the evidential status of choice phenomenology, and the open P3 task "Consonant, not probative" against illusionism adds about 15 words at L151 (2,847, 652). NO edits to agent-causation (12), free-will (2), volitional-control (over its gate) or valence-and-conscious-selection (over). STYLE: no "This is not X. It is Y."; no "load-bearing". ### ✓ 2026-10-04: `concepts/indexical-knowledge-and-identity` L112 says "First-person self-ascription uses no such criteria at all" — drops IEM's grounds-relativity (expand-topic seam, 2026-10-04)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/indexical-knowledge-and-identity.md
- **Notes**: Found by deep-review 2026-10-04 of concepts/immunity-to-error-through-misidentification (seam lens; review at /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/reviews/deep-review-2026-10-04-immunity-to-error-through-misidentification.md). The expand-topic commit ccbcc7b147 added at L112: "First-person self-ascription uses no such criteria at all, which is why it enjoys [[immunity-to-error-through-misidentification|immunity to error through misidentification]]; ...". Universal "first-person self-ascription" is false: IEM is relative to grounds, and self-ascriptions made on observation or testimony use criteria and are open to misidentification (Wittgenstein's "use as object", Blue Book p. 67; SEP "Self-Consciousness" §2.3: "IEM is always relative, then, to the grounds on which a judgement is based"). The IEM article's own lead says "some first-person judgements ... relative to the grounds they rest on". FIX (word-neutral): "First-person self-ascription" → "Introspective self-ascription" (or "Self-ascription of current experience", Strawson 1966 p. 165's scope). Keep the rest of the sentence, including "since every account of the first person predicts that immunity, it cannot by itself answer the prior question", which is correctly calibrated. LENGTH: 2,740 by analyze_length at 12:0xZ (concepts hard 3,500, gate >=); the fix is ±1 word. Re-locate by quoted text. Do not edit other pages. Set ai_modified from the real clock; sync and verify both trees.

### ✓ 2026-10-04: Write article on the divided will — partitioned and distributed rivals to the Map's selector, Ainslie's organ-model test, and why akrasia sharpens the luck objection
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/the-divided-will.md
- **Notes**: Write from the research note at /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/research/partitioned-and-distributed-minds-as-rivals-to-a-unitary-selector-2026-10-04.md; read it IN FULL first, especially §Verdict, §The Objection at Full Strength, §Map Relevance and §Corpus Seams. TITLE "The Divided Will: Partitioned and Distributed Rivals to the Map's Selector"; slug the-divided-will (free in obsidian/, archive/, hugo/content/ on 2026-10-04). `topics:` must be bare slugs and non-empty (e.g. akrasia-and-weakness-of-will, free-will). ACCESS LEVELS: quote only [S] spans, exactly as the note gives them and with its page labels (Davidson 1982 = *Problems of Rationality* 2004 reprint pp. 171–185; Davidson 1970 = *Essays on Actions and Events* p. 42; Ainslie = *Breakdown of Will* 2001 pages; Korsgaard = *Creating the Kingdom of Ends* 1996 reprint pp. 369–383, with the original *PPA* 18(2) 1989 pp. 101–132 pagination unchecked; Minsky by section only, §1 Prologue and §4.2). Use [A] items only as abstract wording, attributed as such: Thaler & Shefrin 1981, Metcalfe & Mischel 1999, McClure et al. 2004, Kable & Glimcher 2007, Daw et al. 2005, Evans & Stanovich 2013, Keren & Schul 2009, Melnikoff & Bargh 2018, Kirby & Guastello 2001, Hofmeyr et al. 2011, Ainslie's 2005 précis and 2021 *BBS* paper, Altehenger 2021. Use [2nd] items (Pears 1984, Rorty 1988, Talbott, Henden, Heather) only as the SEP characterises them. Quote nothing from *Picoeconomics* (1992), Ainslie & Haslam 1992, Elster's introduction or Schelling, none of which was read. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS: "surd" comes from Davidson 1970 ("How Is Weakness of the Will Possible?", p. 42), NOT from "Paradoxes of Irrationality" (1982). Davidson's partition is explicitly non-homuncular ("not one that demands a little agent in the division", p. 185), so never present the parts as little agents. Never attribute the "battlefield" / "little woman, man, or child" picture (p. 171) to Davidson as his view: he describes it in order to set it aside. "Deception and Division" is 1986, in Elster (ed.), *The Multiple Self*, pp. 79–92; the 1985 date is an unverified lead. Ainslie's p. 139 verdict on the organ model and his p. 125 table are his own scoring, so present them as advocacy. The only independent tests are the two small bundling studies (Kirby & Guastello 2001; Hofmeyr et al. 2011, whose authors write "If replicated"), at tier *live hypothesis*. Do not say that Ainslie classes Bratman as resolute choice; he discusses them together (p. 81). The framing that McClure and Kable & Glimcher contradict each other is an unverified lead, so report only each abstract's own claim. Correct the outer review's phrasing: the akratic agent-cause selects against the agent's ALL-THINGS-CONSIDERED RANKING, not "against all of the agent's reasons", because the akratic has a reason for x and none for x-rather-than-y (Davidson 1970 n. 25). The contrast with Kane's torn decisions is a lead: check Kane before leaning on it, or omit it. TIER VERDICT (P-M1), stated in the lead: the fragmentation data (preference reversal, failed resolutions, choice-specific lapses, bundling effects, self-opacity, disavowal) are PREDICTED by partitioned and distributed architectures and only ACCOMMODATED by a unitary selector. They are modest evidence against an organ-style will, and NON-DISCRIMINATING between the Map's selector and a physical arbitration process. The selector has framework-internal coherence only, nothing found supports it over its rivals, and Tenet 5 at most blocks a parsimony dismissal without ever counting for it. INCLUDE: (1) the candidate-selection trilemma: the selector rubber-stamps the supplied values (≈ P-Q3's nearness to epiphenomenalism), or relocates the surd into the subject, or tracks felt valence and so supplies the medium of choosing but not the explanation; (2) interface relocation as always available and therefore non-discriminating; (3) CONSTRAINT: never describe the selector as a faculty of general strength; (4) the luck objection at its starkest, LEFT UNANSWERED (as akrasia L100 leaves it) unless one reply is argued in full; (5) Korsgaard's practical unity as the rival framing, which leaves unity of agency giving the posit no support. GUARDS: never present felt unity of agency, felt ownership or co-conscious pulls as evidence of a unitary subject (Kant page first exposure; Korsgaard p. 377). Do NOT settle the Tenet 3 quantifier (actual vs capacity), which is referred to the operator (todo NEEDS-HUMAN (foundations) 2026-08-17); stay in the "available, not actual" register of tenets#^tenet-3-standing. Make no universal claim that all consciousness is causally efficacious. Leave the bi-aspectual tension between aspects and a persisting subject OPEN. Give the P-SC2 multi-subject pointer one sentence at most, marked speculative. STYLE: no "This is not X. It is Y."; no "load-bearing"; include a Relation to Site Perspective section; set ai_modified from the real clock (UTC, unquoted). LENGTH: target 2,600–3,000 words by tools.curate.length.analyze_length (topics soft 3,000, hard 4,000, gate >=). The topics section stood at 342/360 on 2026-10-04 by tools.evolution.state.count_section_files; re-measure. ACCESS LEVELS: quote only [F] and [S] items, with the note's page labels (Shoemaker, McDowell and Reid pages are reprint/edition pages as labelled; Reid = Woozley abridged ed. 1941); [A] items only as abstract wording, attributed as such; quote nothing from Evans (known only via Parfit n. 15) - paraphrase; Shoemaker, Penelhum and McDowell are snippet-verified - quote only the spans the note gives. QUOTE-SUBJECT GUARDS: McDowell p. 375 'Here, surely, it may be said, we have quasi-memory in the form in which Reductionists want it' voices an OPPONENT - never present it as McDowell's view; Parfit p. 222 'In memory we are directly aware of our own identity through time...' is Parfit voicing a possible reading of Butler, not Parfit's or Butler's own words. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS (coiner verdict): 'quasi-memory' is Shoemaker's term (1970, 'as I shall use the term', reprint p. 24; Parfit p. 516 n. 13 'I follow SHOEMAKER (2)'); credit Penelhum 1970 with the same idea under the name 'retrocognition' (pp. 85, 88) and never say Penelhum used the word 'quasi-memory'; Parfit popularised it (Jane-Paul case, sec. 80) - never write 'Parfit's quasi-memory' or 'Parfit introduced'; avoid an unqualified 'coined' (no earlier-use search was run); do not assert which of Shoemaker/Penelhum came first within 1970; do not attribute the 'q-memory' abbreviation (Parfit 1971 unread); the SEP Memory citation list is alphabetical and is no evidence of priority. Do not repeat outer-review-2026-05-10's backwards framing: quasi-memory shows memory-continuity need NOT presuppose identity. Locke = II.xxvii.13 (Nidditch numbering). MAP POSITION AT HONEST STRENGTH (P-M1 tiers): compatible, no support either way - the Map posits its subject rather than inferring it (Kant page L35), so it loses no premise, but memory, even from the inside, cannot certify which subject had an experience; the Map is committed to quasi-memory's COHERENCE via P-I4 (a copy's states are 'full quasi-memories', Parfit p. 295) - label this the Map's own reconstruction; the coherence critics give a dualist no ally (McDowell's persisting subject is 'a person, of whose continued life that continuity is, precisely, an aspect'; Nichols ties memory-as-evidence to animalism) - say so; never present felt mine-ness or the pastness quale as evidence of a persisting subject (Kant page first exposure); memory remains only defeasible evidence that does not discriminate the posit from rivals (P-I1 grade D). The aspects-versus-persisting-subject tension stays OPEN - do not resolve it. Make no universal claim that all consciousness is causally efficacious. LENGTH/TIER: concept page with a Relation to Site Perspective section; target 2,200-2,700 words (concepts soft 2,500, hard 3,500, gate >=). Section concepts 344/360 at 2026-10-04 by tools.evolution.state.count_section_files - re-measure. INTEGRATION LOCI (measured 2026-10-04 with tools.curate.length.analyze_length; headroom = hard-1-count): voids/inaccessible-past L63 (2,786/3,000, headroom 213) - fix 'Parfit's quasi-memory' to Shoemaker's term (1970), which Parfit made central, plus a link; topics/personal-identity L101 (4,043/4,000, headroom -44, OVER its gate: net-negative edits only) - replace 'marking a memory as genuinely *yours*' with a piped wikilink to the new page on the words 'presenting a memory as *yours*' (-1 word); topics/kants-paralogisms-and-the-maps-subject L86 (3,401/4,000, headroom 598) - 1-2 sentences: Butler's non-transfer premise ('the same property cannot be transferred from one substance to another') is what A363-364n denies, and Parfit's relay passage (p. 223, n. 17) cites Locke and Kant, plus a link; concepts/parfit-reductionism L41 (2,371/3,500, headroom 1,128) - 80-150 words on Parfit's sec. 80 reply to Butler and the Map's acceptance of coherence (discharges outer-review-2026-05-10-claude-opus-4-7 Priority item 5); topics/phenomenology-of-memory-and-the-self L127 (3,996/4,000, headroom 3) and concepts/mine-ness L124 (3,498/3,500, headroom 1) - zero-word piped wikilinks only; do NOT edit positions/ (the register needs a dated Updated note; leave P-I4 to positions-evolve); archive/voids/past-self-void L96 carries the same misattribution - optional fix. ### ✓ 2026-10-04: `topics/the-observer-witness-in-meditation` L157 says S19_137 had "no sham trial" and was "untrained" — both wrong per Vesuna et al. 2020 and Parvizi et al. 2021

TWO DEFECTS: (1) "with no sham trial for him" is FALSE. Vesuna et al. 2020 Nature 586:87–94 (PMC7553818, same patient, Methods "participant number S19-137/SD056") says: "Stimulations through these spontaneously oscillating PMC contact sites evoked a dissociative aura 11 out of 13 times ... Only one sham stimulation elicited report of an aura; this one report followed a real stimulation that had elicited a strong aura." The sham checks the dissociative-aura REPORT only. No witness-profile measure (non-reactivity, non-judging) was taken, so never write "sham-controlled for the witness". (2) "untrained" asserts what the source only fails to report: Parvizi et al. 2021 PNAS has 0 hits for "meditat" and "training". Write "with no reported contemplative training" or drop the adjective. Suggested replacement (about +14 words): "...in a single epilepsy patient with no reported contemplative training; only one of his sham stimulations drew a report of the state, and no one measured his attitude toward it, while ultrasound...". Keep the link to brain-stimulation-and-the-witness-mode. CONSTRAINTS: edit L157 ONLY. Do NOT touch the held Tenet 3 quantifier loci L36, L38, L42, L58, L65, L89, L111, L147, L169, L193 (NEEDS-HUMAN 2026-08-17). Do not use S19_137's "if I wanted to take control, I could have" to argue either quantifier reading. ### ✓ 2026-10-04: `topics/responsibility-gradient-from-attentional-capacity` L61 grades control by the capacity to "hold that recognition stable against competing impulses" but never separates incapacity from weakness of will, so akratic agents read as excused (Claude 2026-10-04)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/responsibility-gradient-from-attentional-capacity.md
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-10-04 (Claude Opus 5.5, akrasia audit, §5 row "responsibility-gradient" and recommendation 11, first half). Read the review file's Verification Notes first. Verified on disk, L61: "An agent who recognises the right course of action but cannot hold that recognition stable against competing impulses exercises less control than one who can." The page never mentions akrasia or weakness of will (0 hits). A reader can map the sentence onto the akratic, but by Davidson's definition the akratic acts freely and intentionally, with the capacity intact. Watson 1977 ("Skepticism about Weakness of Will", *Philosophical Review* 86(3), 316–339) draws the weakness/compulsion line, and the page's gradient implicitly collapses it. Fix: add one sentence after L61, at most 40 words, saying that the gradient concerns capacity. An agent who could hold the recognition stable but lets it lapse shows weakness of will, not incapacity, and is not thereby excused. Link [[akrasia-and-weakness-of-will]]; this is the page's first link to it, and a needed inbound link for the akrasia page. Cite Watson 1977 only at that level of generality: JSTOR was not fetched, so attribute no specific argument to him. COORDINATION: the open P2 "`topics/akrasia-and-weakness-of-will` — add the weakness/compulsion boundary …" item (a) handles the akrasia side, revising its L102 deferral to moral responsibility. Do not edit the akrasia page here. NOT TAKEN: recommendation 11's second half. The reviewer's quote "Meister's 2024 analysis of perceptual limits" is stale: commit 7c10860093 (2026-06-04) replaced it, and L45 now reads "drawing on Zheng & Meister's (2025) analysis of conscious throughput". The Sauerbrei & Pruszynski (2025) critique is handled on topics/bandwidth-of-consciousness L103 and L147. Do not reopen L45. Do NOT settle the Tenet 3 quantifier, which is referred to the operator (todo NEEDS-HUMAN (foundations) 2026-08-17). LENGTH: analyze_length gives 2,956 words against a topics hard limit of 4,000 (gate >=), so headroom is 1,043; add at most +45 words. STYLE: no "This is not X. It is Y." constructions; no "load-bearing"; set `ai_modified` from the real clock. Run scripts/sync.py and confirm that the Hugo copy carries the change.

### ✓ 2026-10-04: Write article on immunity to error through misidentification — criterionless self-ascription, why thought insertion leaves it standing, and why it gives the Map's subject no support
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/immunity-to-error-through-misidentification.md
- **Notes**: Write from the research note at /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/research/immunity-to-error-through-misidentification-2026-10-04.md; read it IN FULL first. Its quotes carry access tags: quote only [F] and [S] items, with the note's page labels. [A] items may be quoted only as abstract wording, attributed as such. Quote nothing from Evans (SEP-reported). Paraphrase Pryor only (page images). Never quote the Prosser & Recanati chapter summaries. SLUG `immunity-to-error-through-misidentification`: free 2026-10-04 03:43Z in obsidian/, archive/ and hugo/content/. CONCEPTS 344/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files` at 03:43Z (re-measure at pick time). LENGTH: target 1,900–2,400 words of prose, total under 3,000 by `analyze_length` (concepts soft 2,500 / hard 3,500, gate `>=`). LEAD WITH THE VERDICT AND TIER (P-M1): IEM is relative to the grounds of a judgement. It is *compatible* with the Map's posited subject and gives it *no support*: every reading predicts it (Child's embodied person, Anscombe's non-referring "I", Kant's formal "I", the Cartesian ego), so it discriminates nothing, like P-I1's grade D for felt this-ness. It removes no defeater. It IS a defeater for any inference from criterionless self-reference or felt "whose"-ness to a non-physical subject. NEVER present IEM as evidence that the subject is non-physical. STRUCTURE: (1) the thesis and its variants: absolute vs circumstantial (Shoemaker 1968), de facto (1970), de re vs wh (Pryor), identification-free (Evans, as the explanation); never use "wide vs strict". (2) Thought insertion: Campbell 1999 ONLY through Coliva 2002's report; Coliva, Gallagher 2000 and the SEP's self- vs other-misidentification distinction; Lane & Liang 2011 (abstract); García-Carpintero 2024 (abstract). Verdict open. The case separates ownership from self-identification. Link thought-insertion and do not restate its five readings. (3) Three readings of significance: Cartesian genealogy (Wittgenstein p. 69; Strawson 1966 pp. 164–166; Child 2026, with the bodily-IEM control "an immaterial ego could not have crossed legs"); Anscombe's no-reference reading (the dilemma, "a stretch of one", "ten thinkers thinking in unison", "selves as postulated objects"); Kantian (Strawson; Longuenesse ch. 5 abstract). (4) The Kant page's three exposures restated in IEM terms, LABELLED as the Map's reconstruction. (5) Relation to Site Perspective: Tenet 1 compatible, no support; Tenet 4: the Anscombe form of deflating the vertiginous question, which P-I2 already concedes as conditional, so no new concession; Tenet 3: no bearing, and make no claim that all consciousness is causally efficacious. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS: (a) cite Shoemaker by the 1984 reprint pagination (1968: pp. 8, 9, 15; 1970: p. 46) and give the journal pages in References. (b) Quasi-memory (UPDATED 2026-10-04 08:05Z from research/quasi-memory-and-the-circularity-objection-2026-10-04.md): Shoemaker 1970 introduced the TERM ("Quasi-remembering, as I shall use the term…"; Parfit 1984 p. 516 n. 13 "I follow SHOEMAKER (2)"), while Penelhum 1970 reached the same CONCEPT independently as "retrocognition" (pp. 85, 88); priority within 1970 is unresolved. "Shoemaker's quasi-memory" is safe; avoid an unqualified "coined". (c) Anscombe's p. 30/31 split is by OCR line position: cite Collected Papers vol. 2 pp. 30–32 as a range, or verify first. (d) Wittgenstein exegesis is contested (Wiseman 2019 vs Child 2026): write "on the standard reading". (e) Child 2026 has no page numbers: cite it by section. (f) Coliva 2006 is pp. 403–425 (Crossref), not the SEP's 407. (g) Do not write "no dualist has argued…": that absence is unestablished. (h) Do NOT carry over the 2026-03-20 unobservable-self research note's claim that IEM "resists physicalist reduction". (i) Zahavi & Kriegel: keep the thought-insertion page's 2016 form or omit (the SEP lists 2015). Its L78 "immunity" is an unrelated sense: leave it. (B) concepts/thought-insertion (2,801/3,500, headroom 698, but the two open P3s "Thought insertion: lead, description and clinical-picture fidelity" and "…self-model and agency rivals at full strength" will spend about +217): at most 110 words near L98 or L70 naming IEM and the self- vs other-misidentification distinction, plus a link. (C) topics/consciousness-and-the-ownership-problem (3,189/4,000, headroom 810): one caveat sentence after L84 plus a link; reword L126's "because 'who' is not a physical category" word-neutrally so it no longer infers non-physicality from the absence of "whose". ### ✓ 2026-10-04: `concepts/mental-effort` L84 — "The strength-resource model collapsed under preregistration" is stale: Vohs et al. 2021 (d = 0.06) agrees, but Dang et al. 2025 (d = 0.31–0.35 under an intense manipulation) does not
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/mental-effort.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-04, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Synthesis: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-10-04.md (Gemini leg abandoned; read the synthesis's adjudication table before editing). UPGRADED P2 → P1 (synthesis cluster K1): both reviewers independently say the ego-depletion record is under-reported as one 2016 figure. ChatGPT says the akrasia page "uses the famous 2016 null result but omits later work that complicates both the negative and positive sides of its conclusion"; Claude says "the replication crisis is under-reported". The Map routes that currency to this page (2026-08-26 stability note). L84 was re-checked on 2026-10-04 and is unchanged. The original ChatGPT Notes and the Claude fold follow verbatim. From outer review 2026-10-04 (ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, §1 "Hagger et al. 2016" and §6; improvement 9). The reviewer aimed this at the akrasia page. However, the stability note in deep-review-2026-08-26-akrasia-and-weakness-of-will routes depletion currency here: "A future review that wants to expand the replication-crisis material should send it to [[mental-effort]], not here". Verified on disk, L84: "The strength-resource model collapsed under preregistration. The felt-drained sense survives; its supposed referent — a unitary depleting resource — appears not to exist as advertised." Sources (metadata verified at Crossref, abstracts via OpenAlex): (i) Vohs, K. D., Schmeichel, B. J., Lohmann, S., Gronau, Q. F., et al. (2021). A Multisite Preregistered Paradigmatic Test of the Ego-Depletion Effect. *Psychological Science*, 32(10), 1566–1581. https://doi.org/10.1177/0956797621989733. It ran k = 36 labs with N = 3,531 and found confirmatory d = 0.06; the Bayesian analysis found the data 4 times more likely under the null. (ii) Dang, J., Xiao, S., Mao, L., Liu, X., et al. (2025). Revisiting Ego Depletion: Evidence from Multi-Lab Collaborations. *Journal of Pacific Rim Psychology*, 19. https://doi.org/10.1177/18344909251386084. A 30–40 min antisaccade manipulation across 14 samples (N = 2,078) gave d = 0.31 to 0.35 with I² = 0. The authors propose that replicability "may hinge on the intensity of the manipulation". Recast L84: the resource model failed in its standard paradigm, but the effect may be intensity-dependent. Keep the section's real point (felt drain does not transparently track a unitary resource) only as far as the evidence now supports it. Optional, one clause: Friese et al. (2017), *Perspectives on Psychological Science* 12(6), 1077–1099, doi:10.1177/1745691617697076, found a training effect of g = 0.30 (bias-corrected .13–.24) with a mechanism that is "poorly understood". LENGTH: analyze_length gives 3,220 words against a concepts hard limit of 3,500 (gate >=), so headroom is 279. Add at most +90 words. Run scripts/sync.py. FOLDED FROM THE SAME-DAY CLAUDE LEG (Claude leg 2026-10-04, Opus 5.5, review file /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/reviews/outer-review-2026-10-04-claude-opus-5-5.md, §2.1; recommendation 15). Convergent on Vohs et al. 2021 (d = 0.06; k = 36, N = 3,531). Recommendation 15 asked for an audit of whether this page carries the depletion material the akrasia page delegates to it. It does (L84), so this task is the whole of that audit. Optional, only within the +90 budget: one clause citing Carter, E. C., Kofler, L. M., Forster, D. E., & McCullough, M. E. (2015). A series of meta-analytic tests of the depletion effect: Self-control does not seem to rely on a limited resource. *Journal of Experimental Psychology: General*, 144(4), 796–815. https://doi.org/10.1037/xge0000083 (title and pages verified at OpenAlex; take the author list from Crossref before citing). The glucose-model critiques (Kurzban 2010, *Evolutionary Psychology* 8(2), 244–259, doi:10.1177/147470491000800208, which exists, and Finley et al. 2019) are leads only. The Claude leg's quotations from them were not verified, so quote nothing from them. SYNTHESIS GUARD (/combine-outer-reviews 2026-10-04): the two legs pull in opposite directions, so the recast must carry both. Vohs 2021 (and optionally Carter 2015) strengthen the null in the standard paradigm, while Dang 2025 suggests an intensity-dependent effect. Do not let Dang 2025 read as a rescue: ChatGPT itself says it "does not restore the original resource model". The budget is unchanged at +90.

It is referred to the operator (todo NEEDS-HUMAN (foundations) 2026-08-17). Wherever wording touches Tenet 3, stay in the available-not-actual register of [[tenets#^tenet-3-standing]]. KEEP: the two ledger-verified quotes (Protagoras 358b–c via SEP, and Davidson p. 42) verbatim; the 1969/1970 hedge, which is deliberate and was ratified by two prior ledgers; and "distinct faculty" and "effortful" for Holton, whose book abstract on his MIT page says "a distinct faculty of will-power, that is effortful to employ". (1) L86, "Davidson builds irrationality into the definition (the agent violates continence for no reason)", contradicts the article's own L66 definition: none of its three conditions is irrationality. Say instead that Davidson's definition picks out the phenomenon, and the verdict of irrationality comes from the principle of continence (L70). (2) Give "no reason" its qualifier at L86. The SEP (rev. 2025-09-18, raw text grep-verified) reads: "while the agent does have a reason for doing b (p. 42, n. 25), he lacks a reason to do b rather than a." L70's "no reason for the gap" is close, so make L86 match it. (3) L68 says the akratic "forms the all-out judgement favouring *x*, and acts on the latter". The essay's core result is that the akratic does not draw the unconditional conclusion that his all-things-considered judgement supports. That the action expresses a contrary all-out judgement is a reconstruction, so label it one. (Lead, unverified: Davidson's "Intending" (1978; Essays pp. 83–102, listed in the SEP bibliography) identifies intentions with all-out judgements. Cite it only if you verify it.) (4) References L122: Hare ch. 5 "Backsliding" is pp. 67–85, not 67–86. Crossref's publisher deposit 10.1093/019881092x.003.0005 gives page "67-85", and ch. 6 "A Moral Argument" starts at p. 87. This supersedes the 2026-08-26 ledger's 67–86. (5) L84: change "The post-Davidson consensus says yes" to "The dominant post-Davidson view says yes". The SEP presents a live debate (Bratman's Sam case, rational akrasia, Holton). (6) The L78 Holton paragraph says "the effortful, trainable capacity" and "What survives is Holton's structural claim—that resolutions can be dropped over-readily and that [holding them] is a distinct, trainable capacity". "Trainable" is not in Holton's abstract, and nothing cited shows that it survived. See Friese, M., Frankenbach, J., Job, V., & Loschelder, D. D. (2017). Does Self-Control Training Improve Self-Control? A Meta-Analysis. *Perspectives on Psychological Science*, 12(6), 1077–1099. https://doi.org/10.1177/1745691617697076. Its abstract, verified via OpenAlex, gives g = 0.30 with bias-corrected estimates of .13 to .24, and says "The mechanisms underlying the effect are poorly understood". Recast: the depletion result tested a resource model, not Holton's conceptual account, so it leaves the structural claim standing without confirming it. Whether the capacity is trainable or domain-general is unsettled; cite Friese 2017. Stability note from deep-review-2026-08-26: ONE calibrating passage only. The wider depletion currency (Vohs 2021, Dang 2025) belongs to concepts/mental-effort, which has its own task, not here. (7) L58: the quoted "have and not have" is in neither the SEP nor Ross's translation. The MIT Classics VII.3 text (grep-verified) reads "having knowledge in a sense and yet not having it, as in the instance of a man asleep, mad, or drunk". Either quote Ross and add "trans. W. D. Ross" to the Aristotle reference, or drop the quotation marks. In the Plato reference, note that the quoted translation is the SEP's. (8) RTSP L92: "If selection were a transparent read-off of the agent's best judgement, Davidson's gap … would have nowhere to open" answers a position no serious rival holds. Replace this foil with the strongest rival. A distributed architecture (several valuation systems on different timescales, habitual vs goal-directed control, temporal discounting, conflict monitoring, later verbal report) predicts judgement/action divergence without any single selector, and recurrent divergence is prima facie evidence AGAINST a unitary personal controller. Also face the regress: a selector with its own reasons and judgements reopens "why did it select what it judged worse?", so the selector relocates Davidson's gap rather than closing it. Concede what the rival explains. Apply the direct-refutation discipline: show a defect in the rival on its own terms, name an unsupported foundational move, or honestly mark the framework boundary. Write it in natural journal-quality prose (writing-style guide, "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose") and expose no mode labels in the article. If no in-framework reply works, say plainly that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than it looked. Do NOT strengthen the selection model's evidential claim. The 07-09 and 08-26 stability notes forbid it, and the reviewer's own remedy keeps akrasia a compatibility case. (9) L100: "What the Map adds—that the controller is genuinely non-physical—rests on its independent arguments (the hard problem, the conceivability argument)". Those arguments support non-reduction at most. They do not show that consciousness is the selecting controller, and epiphenomenalist dualism accepts them. Name the further premise: Tenet 3's interactionist commitment is a separate posit, not a consequence of the hard problem. (10) The RTSP says "interface" but never says whether Minimal Quantum Interaction or No Many Worlds is engaged. Add one sentence saying akrasia engages neither and gives no evidence for a quantum locus. (11) L104 (Tenet 5): keep "simplicity is an unreliable guide", but add that explanatory scope and fit still discriminate among the four positions. Do not codify a parsimony doctrine; that question is an operator item (todo NEEDS-HUMAN (doctrine) 2026-09-19). Run scripts/sync.py after editing and confirm that the Hugo copy carries the change. SEP note 11 (raw notes.html, grep-verified) reads: "not only does he fail to draw an AO conclusion in favor of a; he actually draws an AO conclusion in favor of b. This implication of Davidson's treatment emerges with greater clarity from Davidson 1978, in which he identifies intentions with all-out or unconditional evaluative judgments." Say the all-out judgement favouring x is an implication of Davidson's treatment, made explicit in "Intending" (1978; *Essays on Actions and Events* pp. 83–102), and add that essay to References. Item (4), Hare 67–85, is convergent (OpenAlex record for 10.1093/019881092x.003.0005: pages 67–85). Item (5) is convergent; extend it to L64, "Davidson's achievement is to show that akrasia is possible without being incoherent", which states a contested result as settled. SEP §3.1 (raw text): "Most philosophers writing after him, while acknowledging his pathbreaking work on the issue, think he has not." Reword L64 as Davidson's aim or argument, and name Bratman's Sam (Bratman 1979, p. 156; SEP-verified: Sam thinks it best to abstain, "Still, I'm drinking") as the standard objection, in at most 40 words. Item (6) is convergent: the Claude leg adds that "trainable" belongs to the strength model itself. The NDPR review of Holton's book (raw HTML, grep-verified) says Holton argues that the effort of sticking with resolutions "uses up the power that it exercises, analogously to the way physically resisting a physical force uses up muscular energy". So the depletion result bears on Holton's own account of the capacity, not just on one strand of the evidence; keep item (6)'s "leaves the structural claim standing without confirming it". Item (7) is convergent (the Claude leg also notes that the quoted phrase drops "mad"). Add: name the SEP's Protagoras translation in the Plato reference, E. Hamilton & H. Cairns (eds.), *The Collected Dialogues of Plato*, Princeton University Press, 1961, pp. 308–352 (SEP bibliography, raw text). Optional and word-neutral: L58's "sweet things should be avoided" is a gloss on Ross's "the universal opinion ... forbidding us to taste" (1147a, MIT text), so drop its quotation marks or mark it as an illustration. Items (8), (9) and (11) are convergent (Claude §4 rows 2, 4 and 7). NEW ITEMS FROM THE CLAUDE LEG, each verified: (12) L56, "while trying to preserve what he calls the kernel of truth in the Socratic paradox". Aristotle never calls it that: "kernel" has 0 hits in Ross's NE VII (MIT Classics text). The phrase came in quotation marks from the research note (research/akrasia-and-weakness-of-will-2026-07-09.md L52), a secondary gloss. At 1147b Ross has "the position that Socrates sought to establish actually seems to result". Drop "what he calls" (for example "to preserve what is true in the Socratic paradox") or quote the 1147b sentence. (13) The L70 Davidson p. 42 quotation. This AMENDS the KEEP clause above: keep the words, but fix three defects. The SEP raw text reads: As Davidson puts it, if we ask "what is the agent's reason for doing [b] when he believes it would be better, all things considered, to do another thing, then the answer must be: for this, the agent has no reason" (p. 42). (i) The article puts "if we ask" inside its quotation marks, although it sits outside the SEP's, and Davidson's own wording has not been checked. Open the quotation at "what is". (ii) The article's ellipsis removes ", all things considered,". That qualifier marks the belief as a conditional judgement, the very distinction L68 explains. Without it the quoted belief reads as unconditional, which on Davidson's account the akratic does not hold. The 2026-08-26 ledger called this ellipsis "faithful"; it changes the meaning. Restore the qualifier. (iii) "[b]" is the SEP's editorial substitution and clashes with the article's x/y notation. Change the bracket to "[x]" and cite "(p. 42, as quoted in Stroud & Svirsky 2025)", since the text was taken from the SEP. Net about +6 words. (14) L98: "**Holton** explains it as ordinary, empirically studied willpower failing to hold a resolution". Here "it" is akrasia (the previous bullet reads "Davidson explains akrasia"), but Holton explains weakness of will and distinguishes it from akrasia. SEP §3.3, "Changing the Subject" (raw text): "Weakness of will as the untutored understand it is not akrasia (if we reserve that term for action contrary to one's better judgment)". The article's own L38 and L74 get this right; L98 contradicts them. Fix: "Holton explains *weakness of will*, which he distinguishes from akrasia, as ..." (+5 words). Optional and word-neutral: at L104 ("Holding the four positions open"), note that Holton's reconception changes the subject rather than answering the same question. (15) Extends item (8). Name the concrete mechanisms the distributed rival already supplies: Mele's "the motivational force of a want may be out of line with the agent's evaluation of the object" (SEP-quoted; Mele 1987) and Ainslie's "hyperbolic delay discounting" (Ainslie 1992, 2001; SEP-verified). Any non-transparent mechanism accommodates Davidson's gap, so that the gap opens is a weak constraint, not structural support. (16) L92's last clause equates "whether valence guides selection or is idle to it" with "whether the controller's output tracks the agent's evaluations", and says akrasia is the case where "it does not". But the Map holds that felt value "is what the selection tracks" (topics/the-steelman-for-value-blind-selection L39), and valence-and-conscious-selection L49 already says the value-sensitive horn accommodates akrasia "only if the currency is present felt pull rather than considered judgement". Akrasia is therefore no case of idle valence: on the Map's own thesis, selection tracks present felt pull against considered judgement. Reword the clause, keeping the word count roughly the same, so that it matches valence L49, and link that page's horn analysis. (17) The agent-causal luck problem. The Map's free-will position is agent-causal (topics/free-will L66 and L80; concepts/agent-causation L97, "## The Luck Objection"). An agent-cause that selects against all of the agent's own reasons is the luck objection in its starkest form, and the RTSP never asks how its own metaphysics handles the case it surveys. Add at most 50 words next to item (8)'s regress and link [[agent-causation#the-luck-objection]]. Do not claim the objection is answered unless agent-causation's reply carries over. NOT TAKEN from the Claude leg (see its Verification Notes): the lens-hedge and Tenet 3 filing charges (L100–L102 already disclaim evidential weight; items 8–10 handle the residue), softening "Harean denial" (the SEP's own §1 heading is "Hare on the Impossibility of Weakness of Will"), and moving Map self-citations out of References (a corpus convention). SYNTHESIS ADDITIONS (/combine-outer-reviews 2026-10-04; each verified on the live page, unchanged since 4b6f03fe66). BUDGET RAISED AGAIN: at most +400 words net (supersedes both the +300 and the +380 above), for item (18). (18) NEW, CONVERGENT, previously unminted (ChatGPT §1 "Plato's Protagoras"; Claude 1.2). L50 states "On his intellectualist picture, to judge a course of action best just *is* to be most motivated toward it" as if it were the content of the passage. Both reviewers call this a modern reconstruction, and both say the denial is argued through a hedonist premise and the measuring art. Verified: Jowett's translation (Project Gutenberg #1591, grep-verified) states the conclusion conditionally: "if the pleasant is the good, nobody does anything under the idea or conviction that some other thing would be better and is also attainable, when he might do the better", and the preceding argument turns on "the art of measurement". SEP note 3 (raw notes.html, grep-verified): "But see inter alia Callard 2014, Liu 2022, and Obdrzalek 2023 for recent debate over Plato's view in the Protagoras." Fix L50 in at most +20 words: say "On a standard intellectualist reading", and add that Socrates argues the denial from the premise that the pleasant is the good, together with an art of measuring pleasures and pains, a premise whose endorsement by Socrates is disputed. Paraphrase it; do not quote Jowett, because the page's 358b–c quotation uses the SEP's translation. You may cite SEP note 3 for the live debate, but do not cite Callard, Liu or Obdrzalek directly (unread). GUARD on item (9): tenets L59 lists epiphenomenalism under Tenet 1's "Rules out", so do not write that epiphenomenalist dualism is compatible with the Map's tenets. The point that survives is narrower. The hard-problem and conceivability arguments L100 cites are accepted by epiphenomenalist dualists too, so they support non-reduction only. Excluding epiphenomenalism is a commitment the Map owns (tenets L55: "a commitment the Map owns, not a result it reports"), and downward causation is "a posit the interface argument leaves open, not a result it secures" (tenets L95, ^tenet-3-standing). Name the controller premise as that further commitment. OPTIONAL, word-neutral, with item (10): L90's "one lens among the framework-neutral accounts above" lets a reader take the lens itself as framework-neutral. DO NOT (synthesis R1): both legs also charge that the lens hedge makes the reading unfalsifiable, and that a datum the page calls neutral should not sit under a Tenet 3 heading. Adjudicated and rejected as stated: L100–L102 already disclaim evidential weight, and an RTSP entry recording relevance without support is the required pattern. Do not delete the Tenet 3 heading or add another disclaimer. Items (8), (10) and (15) carry the residue.

(a) Weakness vs compulsion. L102 defers the question to moral responsibility ("whether akratic acts are free and blameworthy is a further question the Map defers"). Desert can be deferred, but classification cannot: the article's own L84 already places Frankfurt's unwilling addict "nearer compulsion than weakness". Add a short treatment. Watson 1977 (already in References) contests the ordinary "could have resisted" contrast and draws the line by normal capacities of self-control. That is the reviewer's summary; JSTOR was not fetched, so state it only at that level of generality. Gorman 2019 and 2023 treat akrasia, compulsion and attributability (SEP-verified). Building on Watson, Gorman holds that compulsive action aims to eliminate a desire rather than satisfy it (SEP cites Gorman 2019 p. 140). Holton's MIT book abstract (verified) also argues that "addictive temptations are not irresistible". Revise L102 so that it defers desert, not classification. In the RTSP, note that a conscious wish that an irresistible process not occur is no evidence of bidirectional control. (b) Rational akrasia. L86's single sentence ("when the agent's 'better judgement' is itself defective") becomes a short paragraph covering Audi 1990, McIntyre 1990, Arpaly 2000 and Bennett 1974's Huck Finn as discussed by McIntyre, all SEP-verified. Arpaly's example is Emily and the chemistry Ph.D., and she says "An agent's best judgment is just another belief" (Arpaly 2000, p. 512). Distinguish procedural from substantive rationality. Tie this to valence: an act driven by present affect against a defective reflective judgement may be MORE value-sensitive, not less. (c) Schapiro 2021 and 2022 (SEP-verified). Weak-willed action is "abandon[ing] your post as deliberator" (Schapiro 2021, p. 152). Quote it exactly in this bracketed form, not in the reviewer's paraphrase. The SEP adds, in its own summary wording (not a Schapiro quotation, so do not put it in her mouth), that weak-willed action "does not actually engage the will at all". Present this as a rival to reading the datum as a positive conscious selection of the worse option. (d) Aristotle, L58–L60. Add impetuosity vs weakness: "Of incontinence one kind is impetuosity, another weakness" (VII.7, Ross trans.; MIT text grep-verified). Add Neoptolemus as "good incontinence" (VII.2) and the "strong-headed", who resemble the continent man (VII.9). The point: in impetuous cases there may be no formed all-things-considered judgement for selection to diverge from. (e) Holton, L76. Add his irreducibly normative criterion so that strength of will does not reduce to stubbornness (SEP: Holton 1999 p. 259; the broken-leg example is the SEP's own illustration). (f) Preference Void. The akratic datum is usually identified by retrospective report. [[preference-void]] (L40, L108–L111) argues that preference orderings may be constructed at elicitation, and that neither its selection reading nor its reception reading "discriminates" the Map from its rivals. Add a sentence saying the "all-things-considered evaluative state" should be time-indexed and kept distinct from post-choice report, and link [[preference-void]]. "Intention and Weakness of Will." *The Journal of Philosophy*, 96(5), 241–262. https://doi.org/10.2307/2564667 (Crossref-verified; the SEP's p. 259 locator for the "irreducible normative dimension"). NEW: (g) Holton's folk-concept thesis is contested. Neighbours checked by subject: the-quantitative-comprehension-void has no suffering or compassion material (its L75 **Magnitude** paragraph is the point of contact); cross-state-void is intrapersonal; voids-between-minds has no number face; the banked combination-void note is about subject composition, not felt totals; the comparative-phenomenology note is about the missing common metric; concepts/the-ownerless-suffering-argument is about Śāntideva on impartial benevolence, an integration candidate rather than a duplicate. **LENGTH**: voids soft 2,000 / hard 3,000, gate `>=` on `analyze_length`, which COUNTS the reference list. Target 2,300–2,600 total (about 1,900–2,150 prose plus at most 20 references), and never above 2,800. **BUILD**: the note's angle 1 ("the sum no one suffers"). Present the structural face (Lewis), the empirical plateau with both readings, and the regulation face (Cameron & Payne) as the occlusion. Keep the note's three layers distinct (L156–164). Lead with the result. **MAP-CONSISTENCY GUARD (critical)**: the Map REGISTERS near-additive aggregation in [[positions/ai-consciousness-scope|P-AC3]] ("duplicating a suffering subject multiplies the suffering"), adopted in ethics-of-possible-ai-consciousness §Aggregation Under Copy-Multiplicity (L58–66). The article must (a) state that the Map sides with the additive, deficit reading on the moral question, on framework grounds (closed individualism, P-I1) and not on felt evidence; (b) present Lewis's and Taurek's tracking reading as a rival the Map does not adopt; (c) keep the void as the claim that felt magnitude cannot decide between the readings. The felt plateau is therefore no evidence against P-AC3, and P-AC3's own calibration ("empirical discriminability none") is what the void predicts. DROP the note's Tenet 4 line that "the additive branch-count is a calculation with no subject" (L202), because it contradicts P-AC3 and phenomenal-value-realism L257. Keep only the symmetric point that felt magnitude is evidence for neither side. Grade Tenet 1 *compatible, no discriminator*, as the note does (L197). **EMPIRICAL GUARDS**: never say flatly that "compassion collapses". Butts et al. 2019 found no significant group-size effect on empathetic concern, so say felt concern does not SCALE and that helping falls. The identifiable-victim effect is small (r = .05, Lee & Feeley 2016, verified 2026-10-03) and failed Maier et al.'s hypothetical-donation replication. The "begins at two" result (Västfjäll et al. 2014) is fragile. Raiyan 2026 is a single-author preprint: call it suggestive and give it one sentence at most. **ATTRIBUTION GUARDS**: "psychic numbing" was coined by Lifton (1967) for Hiroshima survivors and extended by Slovic, so never credit Slovic with the coinage. The Szent-Györgyi quote is secondary and unreferenced in Slovic 2007: either attribute it "as quoted by Slovic (2007)" or omit it. Lewis is writing theodicy, so take his phenomenological claim, not his consoling conclusion. Quote Lewis only as the note gives him (Macmillan 1947 printing, pp. 103–104). The reading of Sidgwick's parenthesis is the note's, not Sidgwick's, so say so. Taurek 1977 and Parfit 1978 were NOT read: do not quote them, state only Taurek's standard form, and cite Piller 2026 for the restatement. The RETROSPECTIVE face overlaps decision-void's Reconstruction face (L71–73: later choice-blindness work "can shift subsequent preferences"). Target 2,300–2,600 total (about 1,900–2,150 prose plus at most 20 references), and never above 2,800. The last six voids articles created landed at 2,451–2,985 and left their reviewers no room. **BUILD**: the note's angle 1. Three faces (elicitation, retrospective, mark) conjoined at the site the tenets select, with contribution versus reception undecidable from inside. Lead with the result. Distinguish the void from the decision, wholeheartedness, appetitive, confabulation and transformative-experience voids in one short section (note L195 and L214). **CALIBRATION GUARDS**: (1) Tenet 3 reading 1 ("construction is the contribution") must be cast as SELECTION among physically prepared candidate weightings or orderings. selection-only-channel L42 has mind "contributing nothing to the alternative set itself", so never say consciousness generates or produces a preference ex nihilo. Do not extend it to preference reversals: the note's Executive Summary (L42) does, so do not copy that sentence. The note omits the main deflationary rival, the discovered-preference hypothesis. Leads: Cox & Grether 1996 (*Economic Theory* 7: 381–405, doi 10.1007/bf01213657) and Braga & Starmer 2005 (*Environmental and Resource Economics* 32: 55–89, doi 10.1007/s10640-005-6028-0). Characterise its findings only after fetching a source; otherwise state it as a position held, not as a result. (3) On the retrospective face, Sharot, Velasquez & Dolan 2010 is the authors' reply to Chen & Risen 2010, not a settlement. Izuma & Murayama 2013 (*Frontiers in Psychology* 4: 41, doi 10.3389/fpsyg.2013.00041; abstract verified 2026-10-03 via Semantic Scholar) reviews the critique and meta-analyses the effect after addressing it, and may be cited at abstract level. (4) Keep the deflation (Warren et al. 2011; McKenzie et al. 2018) at full strength, as in note L206. **ATTRIBUTION GUARDS**: quote NOTHING from Grether & Plott 1979, Tversky, Slovic & Kahneman 1990, Payne, Bettman & Johnson 1992, Kahneman, Ritov & Schkade 1999, Vlaev et al. 2011, Hausman 2012 or McKenzie et al. 2018. Cite Warren, McGraw & Van Boven as 2011 (print; online 2010) with the DOI. ### ✓ 2026-10-03: Write article on Kant's paralogisms and the Map's subject — the Critical dismantling of rational psychology against the register's determinate subject
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/kants-paralogisms-and-the-maps-subject.md
- **Notes**: Slug `kants-paralogisms-and-the-maps-subject` (free 2026-10-01 04:35Z in obsidian/, archive/ and research/). Topics 338/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files` at 04:20Z (re-measure at pick time). Build the note's Angle 1 (§Potential Article Angles L248–254). LEAD WITH THE RESULT: the Map's subject does NOT fall to the paralogisms because it is POSITED, not INFERRED from the "I think" (`tenets.md` L121–123 and P-I2 already say so; on the Dyck/Proops historical-target reading the Map is not Kant's target) — but it is EXPOSED at three points: (i) felt mine-ness/this-ness used as *evidence* (Strawson 1997: Kant's charge of confusing "the unity and simplicity of … consciousness with a consciousness of unity and simplicity"; P-I1's grade-D calibration already concedes it); (ii) the unity-of-consciousness route to a *simple* subject (`unity-of-consciousness`, `apex/mereology-of-mind`) — the A352 inference the second paralogism targets; (iii) posit (1)'s diachronic clause — A363 ("only a formal condition of my thoughts … does not prove at all the numerical identity of my subject"); the causal-history reply at `topics/personal-identity` L187 does not meet Kant's elastic-ball series, so it reduces to the haecceity posit. WHAT ESCAPES: P-SC1's subject index inside P(O | C, X) — a subject determined through its effects is empirical psychology, the route Kant leaves open (B421) — but only on Tenet 3's available-not-actual standing: COMPATIBLE now, suggestive only with a conditioned-register signature, discriminating only with a subject-indexed one. COSTS of the Kantian rejoinder the page must record: P-I1/posit (1) can never be presented as *following from* unity or continuity of consciousness; Tenet 4 loses any appearance of independence from subjecthood (P-I2 registers this); Kant's own B420 agnosticism and Ameriks's modest reading mean the posit is not refuted. Then the four paralogisms with A/B text (B-edition restructuring noted), the historical target (Wolffian rational psychology — bridge to `eighteenth-century-influx-debate` §The Pre-Critical Kant L75–81), and the contemporary dualist rejoinders (Priest's *A Priori Subjects* manuscript, Hasker 2012, Lowe 2012 — the only full-text ones found; Swinburne proxy Kant-zero). Readings of Kant are CONTESTED HISTORIOGRAPHY (Ameriks modest vs strong readings; Dyck/Proops on the target) — label per evidential-status-discipline's 2026-09-30 paragraph; the Map reconstruction label on the three-exposure mapping. **Note's Gaps propagate**: Critique quotes grep-verified in Guyer & Wood (PDF) and Meiklejohn (Gutenberg #4280) — normalise to ONE translation in the article and cite A/B numbers; Kitcher 2024 full text (Crossref says Kitcher — a summariser mis-attributed it; cite Kitcher); 21 metadata-only sources (Ameriks, Dyck, Proops, Rosefeldt, Kitcher 1982/1990/2011, Longuenesse, Brook, Allison, Grier, Zahavi, Swinburne…) cite WITHOUT quotation; Chalmers 1996 NOT consulted — do not assert the "contemporary heir" lead; SEP's A355 and the A352 "Achilles" phrase were not re-found — do not quote them; Priest's publication status unknown — cite as manuscript. Quote only spans the note marks grep-verified. Reciprocals (the note's L256–270 table has measured headroom): most hosts are AT OR OVER their gates — `topics/personal-identity` 4,045/4,000, `positions/individuation-and-subjecthood` 3,938/2,500, `positions/subject-census` 2,493/2,500, `voids/self-opacity` 3,072/3,000 — piped wikilinks over existing text ONLY, zero added words, re-measure each and confirm unchanged; `topics/consciousness-and-the-metaphysics-of-individuation` (3,143/4,000) and `apex/mereology-of-mind` (measure; apex gate 5,000) and `eighteenth-century-influx-debate` (3,714/4,000) may take one sentence each; `concepts/pudgalavada` and `topics/indexical-identity-quantum-measurement` measure first. Target 2,400–2,900 body words; topics hard gate 4,000. `topics:` bare slugs; ai_modified from the real clock; changelog before sync; sync; validate. ### ✓ 2026-10-03: Write article on Anton syndrome and the sincere report of seeing — what the blind patient's report of sight shows about first-person authority
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/anton-syndrome-and-the-sincere-report-of-seeing.md
- **Notes**: Slug `anton-syndrome-and-the-sincere-report-of-seeing` (free 2026-10-02 23:35Z in obsidian/, archive/ and hugo/content/). The research slug says "absent experience", but the evidence does not settle whether the experience is absent (reading A) or present and misclassified (reading B), so the article title must not presuppose absence. Topics 339/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files` at 23:35Z (re-measure at pick time). Target ~2,800–3,400 words (topics soft 3,000 / hard 4,000, gate `>=`). Build the note's Angle 1 (§Potential Article Angles). LEAD WITH THE RESULT: Anton leaves Layer 1 (`phenomenal-authority-and-first-person-evidence` L102, a GLOBAL existence claim) standing; it shows MODAL-existence reports ("I am having visual experience") are fallible under pathology, so they carry Layer-2-grade authority; authority attaches to seemings, not reports; Chalmers 1995 restricts authority to unimpaired rational systems by name. Tiers: Tenet 1 compatible/non-discriminating; Tenet 3 compatible, correlation reply already conceded (tenets L101); Tenets 2 and 4 not invoked. Centrepiece case: Swartz & Brust 1984 (belief in sight only during hallucinosis the patient recognised as unreal), held at ABSTRACT strength. Sourcing limits: most clinical sources are abstract-only [A]; Macpherson 2010 (Phil. Issues 20:226–265, the one paper wholly on Anton and introspection) is METADATA ONLY, so try to read it before writing §5 and attribute nothing to it otherwise; Allen-Hermanson 2015/2017 abstract only. Do NOT: repeat Lane & Liang's "delirium tremens" (the primary abstract says alcoholic hallucinosis, "otherwise mentally normal"); count Rodríguez 2024's enucleated patient as a report of current sight (he expects vision to RESUME); call McDaniel 1991 total-blindness denial (it was MONOCULAR). Integration edits listed in the note, with measured headroom: anosognosia page (+Kletenik/Monai supramodal correction to the "one for each domain" sentence), constitutive-vs-referring-observation (seemings vs reports), organizational-invariance (Chalmers's blindness-denial reply + Mogensen n. 18), blindsight L171, perceptual-reality-monitoring-void (Perky's clinical limit), erasure-void L52 ("same lesion" over-general for Anton; pipe-link the unnamed sentence); cotard-delusion and higher-order-theories take zero-word piped links only; phenomenal-authority is over its gate, so at most a length-neutral rewording of the L172 table row.

### ✓ 2026-10-03: Write article on the ignorance hypothesis — Stoljar's epistemic view, why it is type-C rather than type-F, and what Tenet 5 commits the Map to grant it
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/ignorance-hypothesis.md
- **Notes**: Slug `ignorance-hypothesis` (free 2026-10-03 10:17Z in obsidian/, archive/ and hugo/content/). Title "The Ignorance Hypothesis". Concepts 343/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files("concepts")` at 10:17Z (re-measure at pick time). LENGTH: concepts soft 2,500 / hard 3,500, gate `>=`; `analyze_length` counts the reference list, so keep the TOTAL at or below ~3,000 (prose ~2,000–2,500 plus ~12 references). READ FIRST the research note's §Verdict, §Reconciling the Filings, §Map Relevance and §Corpus Seams, and concepts/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism (it owns the A/B/C taxonomy, the collapse argument and the reply-routing table; link `[[type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism#Why Type-C Collapses]]` and restate none of them). SPINE: Stoljar's formulation and E1/E2 structure in his own terms ("the epistemic view"; later "non-standard physicalism"); where it sits (type-C, not type-F); why it is the hardest opponent for the Map's premise-sharing cluster (it undercuts from the PHYSICAL side, survives the failure of every phenomenal-concept variant, and Tenet 3's selector does nothing against it, per P-D2); what does not answer it; what does reach it; Tenet 5 parity; tier. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS (each verified in the note; do not loosen): (1) Do NOT write that Chalmers files the ignorance hypothesis under type-F. "Consciousness and its Place in Nature" (2003) predates the 2006 book and files Stoljar 2001 (the object-based/intrinsic-property expansion) under type F. The 2006 view is type-C by Chalmers's own type-C definition ("perhaps because we do not currently grasp all the required physical truths"), by Stoljar's own 2020 self-filing (Noûs 54(2), abstract: "type‐C Materialism") and by McClelland 2020. When attributing a type-C filing to CHALMERS, say "on Stoljar's reading" (Four Kinds n. 29 is a forecast, not a report). "Collapses into F" is Chalmers's argument, not a filing. (2) Quote the formulation as the OUP chapter abstract's wording ("we are ignorant of a type of non-experiential truth relevant to the nature of experience") and label it as such; the book body was NOT read. The slugs-and-tiles example, the "John is a number" reply and the late "appears possible" reading of conceivability are relayed by Papineau's NDPR review: attribute them to that review. The p. 10 quote is relayed by McClelland. (3) Four Kinds of Russellian Monism quotes are from the author's preprint (ANU), with the preprint's pagination; cite the Kriegel volume, pp. 17–39. (4) Kind's three objections ("advertised effect", "you're not done", burden of proof) are known from Stoljar's reply abstract (Routledge 2023, pp. 193–200). Attribute them as summarised there. (5) Papineau is a Type-B physicalist, and his "hard to see how" objection states an intuition that IH predicts. Do not present it as decisive; its strongest form is Chalmers's conceptual-hook argument read on the consciousness side. (6) "Stoljar's epistemic physicalism" is the Map's own coinage; use Stoljar's terms and mention the Map label at most once. (7) Doggett & Stoljar 2010 ("Nagel's Footnote Eleven") is co-authored. (8) Alter 2009, Bennett 2009, Gertler 2009 and Levine 2008 are metadata-only: cite them only as existing critiques and report no arguments from them. Stoljar 2015 "Russellian Monism or Nagelian Monism?" is unverified, so do not cite it. (9) The claim that the Map and Stoljar differ on the CONTENT of the ignorance (non-experiential truths versus consciousness-involving selection, Chalmers's route 4 / type-D, physical-completeness L86–L88) is the Map's own analysis; label it so. MUST NOT: answer IH with the acquaintance reply (it concerns phenomenal-side ignorance; IH's ignorance is physical-side); cite the gap's persistence or the "hard to see how" intuition as evidence against IH (IH predicts both); charge IH with unfalsifiability without the symmetry caution (tenets.md calls Tenet 2 a "consistency claim"); claim any tier lift. TIER: *compatible* against IH, not the *suggestive* the routing page L123 gives Type-C generally. The replies that reach IH (conceptual hook; structure-and-dynamics, with Stoljar's three-readings reply and physical-completeness L90's "bet on the method-claim"; the precedent disanalogy via concepts/vitalism; "you're not done") are defeater-removal or dialectical under P-M1 (`[[positions/methodology-and-calibration#^p-m1|P-M1]]`). TENET 5: the Map grants E2 (tenets.md L141 "The apparent simplicity of physicalism could reflect ignorance rather than insight" is the parity premise) and contests E1. Q2: state in one or two sentences that the four-quadrant Q2 entry fits only as a flagged physicalist limit case (epistemic thickness, null mind-side), and that Q2's exclusion debt and Tenet-3 ruling do not attach; Tenet 1 is the operative exclusion. INTEGRATION (lengths by `analyze_length` at 10:2xZ; re-measure; headroom = hard − 1 − count): concepts/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism 2,818 (headroom 681): L75 zero-word pipe on "ignorance hypothesis" plus a Further Reading line. An open P3 ("Fix two two-dimensional-argument errors") lists this page as a secondary file, so touch nothing else there. concepts/physical-completeness 2,703 (796): one-sentence pointer at L90. concepts/russellian-monism 2,949 (550): one Further Reading line. topics/mechanism-costs-dualism-thickness-quadrants 3,303 (696): L85 pipe plus a one-clause flag that the exclusion debt does not attach. topics/four-quadrant-dualism-taxonomy 4,003 (OVER topics hard 4,000): zero-word pipe ONLY at L102 (`[[ignorance-hypothesis|Stoljar's epistemic physicalism]]`). apex/dualism-cartography 5,185 (OVER apex hard 5,000): zero-word pipe ONLY at L69. concepts/mysterianism 3,434 (65): zero-word pipe only, optional. DO NOT EDIT: positions/arguments-for-dualism (2,495/2,500; the P-D1 shift-condition seam belongs to positions-evolve, which requires a dated Updated note), concepts/knowledge-argument (8 headroom), concepts/explanatory-gap (4), concepts/philosophical-zombies (24; open P3), concepts/zombie-master-argument (acquaintance-reply seam recorded for a later task). ### ✓ 2026-10-03: `topics/bergson-and-duration` moves from phenomenological non-spatiality to ontological non-physicality without an argument, and calls the result "independent" support for dualism
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/bergson-and-duration.md
- **Notes**: Apply the direct-refutation discipline: name the unsupported step in natural prose and expose no mode labels. L39 (the lead) says that if lived time resists spatial representation, "consciousness does something physics cannot capture—strengthening the case that the hard problem marks a genuine ontological boundary, not merely an explanatory gap. The Map's [[tenets#^dualism|dualism]] gains independent philosophical support." The RSP Dualism paragraph (L131) repeats the move: "if lived time has qualitative features that spatial description cannot capture, then a complete physical description of the brain leaves out something real about the mind". Two steps are unargued. (a) "Spatial description" is equated with "physical description", but physics represents time dynamically, and Bergson's target is a mode of representation. (b) A phenomenological datum is moved to an ontological conclusion. Rewrite so that durée challenges snapshot-based and spatialising reductive strategies and leaves the explanatory gap where the hard-problem arguments put it. The ontological step should rest on those arguments, which Bergson illustrates but does not supply. Drop "independent". Also check L133 ("This creativity is what the Map means by consciousness exercising causal power … makes such causation intelligible") against tenets.md ^tenet-3-standing, which says causal efficacy is available, not shown actual.

- **Type**: expand-topic

LENGTH: concepts soft 2,500 / hard 3,500, gate `>=`; `analyze_length` counts the reference list, so keep the TOTAL at or below ~3,200 (prose ~2,300–2,700 plus ~14 references). READ FIRST: concepts/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism (it owns the A/B/C taxonomy and the reply-routing table; restate neither) and the research note's §Verdict, §Map Relevance and §Corpus Seams. Build Angle 1 ("Two kinds of primitiveness", §Potential Article Angles). LEAD WITH THE RESULT: Chalmers's 2003 verdict ("embracing primitive identities or strong necessities") names a real cost that Type-B largely concedes on the explanation side (parity: Type-B's explanatory structure "is just like the explanatory structure of property dualism", Chalmers & Jackson 2001); the live dispute is whether an epistemically primitive bridge is an identity or a law; the hinge is Chalmers & Jackson's "Identities are ontologically primitive, but they are not epistemically primitive". The Map's tier against Type-B STAYS compatible: the cost is defeater-removal under P-M1 and raises no tier; it is an input to the routing page's unrun second-order comparison, not a substitute for it. TENET 5 CUTS BOTH WAYS: it discounts Block & Stalnaker's simplicity/IBE case for the identity AND the simplicity strand of Chalmers's case against modal dualism (one modal primitive versus two); only his epistemology-of-modality strand survives it, and that strand is a priori. STATE WHAT THE COST DOES NOT SHOW (the note's list): not that the identity is false; not dualism rather than type-F; nothing specific to interactionism (the dualist psychophysical law is equally primitive, parsimony L83); not independent support (Chalmers 2010: no strong necessities is equivalent to CP-, so P-D1 files the cost in the conceivability cluster); nothing empirical. ATTRIBUTION GUARDS (use the note's verified loci and spans only): "identities need no explanation" is Papineau 1993 p. 180, 1998 p. 379, 2011 p. 9 (his 2002 book is metadata-only); "Identities don't have explanations" is Block & Stalnaker 1999 p. 24; "gappy identity" is Levine, Purple Haze (OUP 2001, not the "MIT Press 2000" entry in Chalmers's bibliography), introduced p. 84, quote only the verified snippet spans; "strong necessities" is named in Chalmers 1999, shortening the 1996 book's "strong metaphysical necessities"; "new wave materialism" is Horgan & Tienson's critics' label, McLaughlin a holder; "theft over honest toil" is Russell 1919. DO NOT repeat zombie-master-argument L102's claim that the coincidence of primary and secondary intensions is the key premise: Chalmers calls it "inessential" (1999) and "not required" (2003). Write "Chalmers's (2007) master argument against the phenomenal-concepts strategy", never a bare "master argument" (the zombie page uses that phrase for the conceivability argument). DISAMBIGUATE in the first section from the primitive identity of subjects (Nida-Rümelin; topics/consciousness-and-the-metaphysics-of-individuation L91). Report Schaffer 2017 at abstract level only, and Kripke's stance as disputed (Chalmers 2003 versus Goff & Papineau). COORDINATION: become the canonical host for the identities-need-no-explanation exchange now split across the-relocation-objection L78/L84 (headroom 1,012), type-identity-theory L71 (headroom 1,177) and topics/parsimony-case-for-interactionist-dualism L75 (headroom 75: zero-word pipe ONLY). After writing, repoint the routing-table rows "A primitive identity is a law under another name" and "Two-dimensional argument" (type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism L89–90; the second now points at kripke-a-posteriori-necessity-argument, which has no two-dimensional content) with zero-word edits; phenomenal-concepts-strategy (headroom 51) and explanatory-gap (headroom 4) take zero-word pipes only. The zombie-master-argument L102 correction is a separate refine, not part of this task.
