### ✓ 2026-10-03: `topics/consciousness-as-activity` claims more than the tenets page, the Map's hard-problem page and its own concessions allow — one calibration pass
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/consciousness-as-activity.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-03, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. The Gemini leg was abandoned, so this is 2/2 of the legs that reported. Read both review files' Verification Notes. ChatGPT: "'Answered' outruns 'available'". Claude: the exclusion answer "claims more than the Map's own Tenets page allows". The synthesis checked every item against the live page, which is unchanged since a5791c646a. Items (1), (2), (3), (4), (6) and (8) hold and both reviewers flag them. Item (5) is convergent on its dynamic-property and non-reductive-property-view half (Claude §3.1, §3.6, §4); "exactly two families" itself is ChatGPT's alone. Item (7) is ChatGPT-only and item (9) Claude-only. Item (10) below is new and convergent. Constraints restated from the Caution field, which the dispatcher does not pass: do NOT edit tenets.md, embodied-cognition, causal-powers or emergence, and do not argue against MWI. Aim for net-neutral length (3011 words, hard 4000). Keep "defeater-removal", the Occam paragraph's 09-26 hard-problem wording (do not revert to "generates the hard problem") and the James/Whitehead divergence in Agent-dependence. Run ONE editor pass covering this task and its P1 source-fidelity sibling below.
  Apply the direct-refutation discipline where an opponent is engaged: show the defect on the opponent's own terms, name an unsupported foundational move, or honestly mark the framework boundary. Do this in natural journal-quality prose (see the writing-style guide's "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose") and expose no mode labels in the article. (1) **Closure "answered"**: L87 ("where it is answered by the selection mechanism") and L130 ("This is where the exclusion problem deferred above ... is answered"). tenets.md L95 says the interface argument shows downward causation *available*, not *actual*. It notes that P-Q10 records no worked toy model, and that articles asserting "real work" or "genuine causal work" "inherit that debt rather than discharge it, and should read no more confidently than this paragraph does". Restate both loci: the exclusion problem relocates to the interface, where the Map assigns it to a hypothesised selection mechanism that makes a non-duplicating mental contribution available but not demonstrated. Link [[tenets#^tenet-3-standing]]. Keep "defeater-removal". (2) **MWI**: L132 says the activity framing "reinforces indexical identity" and that "activities cannot be copied without becoming different activities". An Everettian grants that the post-branch activities are distinct tokens and holds that all of them are real, so the copy point does not answer that view. tenets.md L123 says that if the subject were "treated as a mere process, the indexical objection would weaken correspondingly", and L170 says "branch-relative agency is otherwise available". Restate: the activity framing supports indexical identity only on the non-deflationary performing subject posited under Agent-dependence (L85), and it inherits the dependency recorded at tenets L121–L123. (3) **Agent-causal selection presented as a datum**: L53 lists "agent-causal selection" among the features "physical description cannot capture", beside subjective character and intentionality. But L85 itself says the agent-causal reading is the agency cluster's adoption and that the tenet "commits only to outcome-selection" (see also tenets L151). Remove it from the L53 list, or mark it there as the Map's downstream commitment. (4) **Hard problem misattributed**: L49 says the classic formulation "drawing on Nagel's ... and Chalmers's hard problem—presupposes the property framework. The question is well-formed only if consciousness is something a system *has*." Chalmers (1995, "Facing Up", consc.net/papers/facing.html) poses it about processing and function: "Why is the performance of these functions accompanied by experience?" He also rejects nonlinear dynamics and quantum mechanics as "extra ingredients". The Map's own [[hard-problem-of-consciousness]] frames it the same way (L94 "cascade of neural activity"; L119 "Why doesn't all this processing happen 'in the dark'?"). Restate L49 so the classic question is already posed of processes, and present L55's activity question as a sharpened explanandum, not a reframing with "more conceptual traction". Also make L104 ("strips away exactly the features") and L134 ("trades that question for a more tractable one") consistent with that. (5) **"Exactly two families"** (L47) omits substance dualism, which the Map's own agency cluster leans towards (tenets L53 and L151 are neutral between substance and property dualism). It also omits neutral/Russellian monism, panpsychism, illusionism, and the process physicalism the article later concedes at L126. Name the field compactly, and add that properties can be dynamic or dispositional, so the property/activity contrast is not a static/dynamic contrast (L104 presupposes that it is). (6) **Lead vs concession**: L43 says the shift "helps explain why materialist reduction keeps stalling: the property framing may be aiming its reduction at the wrong category". L126 concedes that process-identity physicalism (Place 1956; Smart 1959) already aims at processes, and that the hard problem stands for it too. That concession undercuts the "wrong category" explanation of the stalling. Also check "aligns naturally" (L43) and "dualism becomes more natural" (L126) against L126's own "neutral ground" conclusion and the RSP opener ("removing obstacles, not ... proving them"). The frontmatter description already says "removes obstacles". (7) **Whitehead and quantum measurement**: L65 says "the Map reads [[quantum-measurement-and-subjective-probability]] as closing on precisely this point", but that page (L130) says only that the selection "resonates with" process philosophy's actual occasions. Reduce this to a thematic analogy, labelled as the Map's conjecture and not a result drawn from Whitehead. (8) **Enactivism gloss**: in Further Reading, L147 ("How enactivism challenges and ultimately reinforces the dualist insight") contradicts the body at L116, which calls this "a framework-boundary difference that enactivism's phenomenological commitments do not settle in the Map's favour". Make the gloss match the body, and check L116's closing "ultimately cannot close" sentence against it.
  Claude review fold (moved here verbatim from its own field by the 2026-10-03 synthesis, because the dispatcher passes only Notes): `reviews/outer-review-2026-10-03-claude-opus-5-5.md` (collected 04:23Z; read its Verification Notes) independently flags items (1), (2), (3), (4), (5), (6) and (8), so these are convergent. For (1) it quotes tenets.md's "should read no more confidently than this paragraph does" (verbatim). For (2) it adds the type/token point: under branching each branch has a continuous token activity with the same pre-split history, which is the standard Parfitian fission reply. Extra loci, all checked on disk: (2) L85 gives agent-causal selection and unity as the reasons for a performing subject. [[where-the-substance-commitment-enters]] names agent causation and indexical identity as the two homes (its `#second-home` section) and never mentions unity. When restating L132, link [[where-the-substance-commitment-enters#second-home]]. (3) The same move appears at L100: "constituting its own content through [[agent-causation|agent-causal]] choice" builds the disputed conclusion into a candidate feature. (4) L49's "This metaphor creates the [[hard-problem-of-consciousness|hard problem]] in its classic form" also conflicts with L126's "The verb shift leaves the hard problem standing". (5) L126's "Properties invite reduction" is contradicted by non-reductive property views (IIT, Russellian monism, panpsychism). L51's "category error" does not mention Ryle, who aimed the category-mistake charge at dualism. At most, add a clause. NEW item (9), verified on disk: **who performs the activity, and is the body a constituent or an interface?** The description and L43 call consciousness "something the organism *does*". But L85 has it "performed by a subject", and L116 says "for the Map, a subject whose activity is realised through that coupling without being identical to it". L112 says, in the Map's voice, that "the body is not merely a vehicle for a conscious mind but part of the activity that constitutes it". [[embodied-cognition]] L142 says instead that the body "provides the **interface** through which consciousness engages the world" and "constrains and shapes experience without necessarily generating it". Make the description and lead name the performer consistently with L85/L116. Either attribute L112's constitution claim to embodied/enactivist theorists or restate it in interface terms. L43's "The Unfinishable Map argues this framing is a mistake" speaks for the whole Map, yet [[causal-powers]] L49 ("present as a feature of phenomenal states") and [[emergence]] L171 ("Consciousness has properties and powers") use property and power talk. Scope the claim to this article's argument. Do NOT edit embodied-cognition, causal-powers or emergence. Rejected Claude loci (do not act on): its perceptual-degradation-and-the-interface quote is from an ARCHIVED page. It quotes interactionist-dualism L125's "Attention is neurally implemented" out of context (that line is about multi-mind collapse).
  Synthesis additions (2026-10-03; each locus verified on the live page). (1), narrowed: L130's closing sentence ("The claim is that this answer is available on the activity framing, not that closure is shown false by definition") is already in the right register. The defect is "is answered" at L87 and L130 without the not-yet-actual qualification and the P-Q10 no-toy-model debt. Bring both up to L130's own last sentence; do not rewrite the section. (4), extra loci, convergent: L93–L95 ("The Hard Problem Reframed") present the activity question as a reformulation with "advantages", and say the property version invites "deflationary ones (consciousness is an illusion)". Claude §4 notes that Dennett's multiple-drafts model and illusionism are process theories. ChatGPT §4 calls the activity question "close to a restatement" of Chalmers's. Recast L93–L95 as a sharpened explanandum, consistent with (4). Nagel nuance: Nagel's own canonical sentence uses possession grammar ("an organism has conscious mental states if and only if there is something…", checked in the 1974 PDF text). Rest the L49 correction on Chalmers, whose formulation is about processing and function, and do not claim that Nagel's wording avoids "has". (10) NEW, convergent: **symmetric accounting of debts.** ChatGPT §11.4: "The article counts the property view's debts without giving an equivalent account of its own." Claude §5: conceptual richness "is just as unable to decide in the other direction". L55 says the activity reading "earns its place by the puzzles it avoids", and L134 books only the Whiteheadian form's debt (the combination problem). The Map's own form, with the performing subject posited at L85, carries its own open questions: what pairs that subject with one brain, and how it persists across selections. [[the-agent-shaped-hole]] applies to it too. Add one clause at L134 (or L55) naming them, without reverting the Occam paragraph's 09-26 wording. (2)/(9) note: both reviewers call L85's second reason (unity "reads more naturally") an intuition, not an argument (ChatGPT §6.3; Claude §6). L85 already marks the performing subject as the Map's posit, so do not add a new defence. The Claude-fold fix below (link the second home, and note that unity is not one of the two homes) is the whole remedy. ### ✓ 2026-10-03: `topics/consciousness-as-activity` — four source-fidelity slips, a stale GNW paragraph and two missing counterexample classes
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/topics/consciousness-as-activity.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-03, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. The Gemini leg was abandoned, so this is 2/2 of the legs that reported. Read both review files' Verification Notes. ChatGPT: the Clark slogan "is not faithful to Clark's terminology". Claude: "predictive processing is defined over generative models". The synthesis checked every item against the live page. Items (1), (2), (3), (4) and (5) hold and both reviewers flag them. (3) is newly counted: Claude's Bergson row calls "placing it alongside other properties like position and charge" "the article's modern gloss presented as Bergson's critique". (6) is convergent on L99/L102 (see the additions) and ChatGPT-only on the passive and unconscious controls. (8)'s bifurcation disclosure is convergent (ChatGPT §10.3 and §12 "Process Philosophy"). Both reviewers also charge that the page blurs experience and consciousness for Whitehead. L63/L65 refute that charge: do NOT act on it. (7) Noë 2009, (9) Kim and (10) provenance are Claude-only. The L118 skill-performance claim is convergent and no longer optional. (11) below is new and convergent. Constraints restated from the Caution field, which the dispatcher does not pass: this is the same file as the P1 calibration task above, so run one editor pass. Length is 3011 words against a hard 4000, so fund additions with trims. COGITATE byline: "Cogitate Consortium (Ferrante, O., Gorska-Klimowska, U., et al.) (2025)", not "Melloni et al.".
  (1) **Clark slogan**: L110 says "brains don't *have* models, they *do* modelling". Clark 2013's own abstract (BBS 36(3), verified via OpenAlex) says prediction "is achieved using a hierarchical generative model". Remove the slogan, or replace it with wording faithful to Clark (an active process run on a hierarchical generative model). (2) **James on effort**: L77 says "If consciousness were merely a property of certain brain states, effort would be inexplicable. Properties don't strain. Activities do." James is stronger evidence against this than for it. *Principles* ch. XI has a section titled "Is Voluntary Attention a Resultant or a Force?", states the effect-theory "as persuasively as I can" and only "inclin[es]" to the cause-theory (Gutenberg 57628, footnote 379, grep-verified). Replace the slogan with the phenomenal datum (effort is felt), and note the rival functional readings (control conflict, resource allocation) and James's own open verdict. (3) **Bergson**: L71's "targets exactly the property framing" goes beyond Bergson, whose target is the spatialisation of time and quantitative multiplicity. Make it "bears on". (4) **Temporal constitution**: L83 says flatly that a time-slice "cannot contain consciousness", which assumes extensionalism. The Map's own [[temporal-consciousness]] L102 favours "modified extensionalism" and says the choice "may be structurally underdetermined". [[temporal-consciousness-structure-and-agency]] L88–L90 presents retentionalism as live. Make L83 conditional on the extensional reading, and optionally cite Singhal & Srinivasan (2021), *Neuroscience of Consciousness* niab020, doi 10.1093/nc/niab020, which combines the cinematic, extensional and retentional models (verified via Crossref and OpenAlex). (5) **GNW currency**: L120 rests "the neural correlates of consciousness look more like dances" on Dehaene & Changeux 2011 alone. Add the 2025 adversarial result. - **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-02, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude] (synthesis cluster 4). Upgraded P2→P1. The shared finding is that self-stultification needs report content to depend on phenomenal content, and that several Map routes do not secure it. ChatGPT §2.1 makes the point for trumping, and §2.5 for bare selection: "consciousness contributes no explained relation between phenomenal content and report content". Claude T3 makes it for value-blind selection and the witness state: "Reports of the witness state would then have to be caused by something other than the state reported. That is precisely the self-stultification structure the Map uses against epiphenomenalism." Adjudication (synthesis): the fork bites on this page because L69 grants authority only "when an experience with the matching causal profile is present". Schaffer's own cases are content-sensitive (a different order from the major yields a different outcome), and L51 already cites Lewis's influence test, so a content-sensitive authority law is available on paper. Its price is a trajectory change whenever experience and base diverge, which brings back the empirical exposure the template was meant to avoid. tenets.md L91 defines Tenet 3's influence as biasing outcomes "toward patterns that express its content". Test that route first, as the original notes direct. Re-verified on the live page 2026-10-03: 0 hits for "self-stultif", and L69, L79 and L86 are unchanged since 2026-09-19, at 3,229 words. Three instances of the same finding are NOT in this task's edit scope; the synthesis records them. First, stapp-quantum-mind L100 (the witness state is "not steering"); the stapp-quantum-mind P2 defers it here and to the quantifier task, but neither edits that file. Third, positions/value-in-selection L42, which already concedes that the bias-without-deviation dilemma sits "close to epiphenomenalism". Original notes follow. From outer review 2026-10-02, §2.1 (novel: not raised in any prior review) and §7.7. The self-stultification argument (`topics/self-stultification-as-master-argument`; [[epiphenomenalism]] "The Self-Stultification Problem") needs the *content* of a report to depend counterfactually on the content of the experience: P(R | do(E), B) ≠ P(R | B). Trumping leaves the physical base counterfactually sufficient (L69) and makes no trajectory change (L79, L87). It can make experience the *authoritative* cause of a report, but not a report whose content would have differed had the experience differed. So a dualism that relies on trumping cannot also count self-stultification as evidence; on that route the anti-epiphenomenalist case has to come from elsewhere. Verified during processing: this page never mentions self-stultification or the content of reports, and epiphenomenalism never mentions trumping. Fix: add this as a further cost in "Costs of the Template" (L79), and qualify the Bidirectional Interaction bullet in "Relation to Site Perspective" (L86, "describes one precise way in which that influence could be realised") to match. Do not restate §2.4: L87 already says the quantum-selection channel is "a distinct and potentially competing mechanism", which covers it. Apply the direct-refutation discipline. First try whether a content-sensitive version of trumping is available (for example, an authority law indexed to phenomenal content). If none is, say in plain language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than it first appears. Do not expose mode labels in the article body. Do NOT edit `topics/self-stultification-as-master-argument`, which tenet-check row 2 owns; the epiphenomenalism reciprocal is part of the 2026-10-02 P1 quantifier task. ### ✓ 2026-10-02: Zombie master argument misdescribes Chalmers's taxonomy and misuses "Type-Q" (L34, L76–82) — override six reviews that ratified it
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/zombie-master-argument.md
- **Notes**: Four defects at L76–82 (with the L34 framing they depend on): (a) L78 "Chalmers's taxonomy covers Type-A and Type-B" is false; Chalmers 2003 has types A–F plus Q, and the response map omits Type-C, which denies premise 1 under ideal conceivability. (b) L78 "'Type-Q' is not standard nomenclature" is false; "type-Q materialism" is Chalmers's own 2003 coinage for the Quinean who rejects the a priori/a posteriori distinction. The page reuses the label for a different position. (c) That position ("accepts zombie possibility while resisting the dualist conclusion") denies premise 3, which the two-dimensional argument assigns to **Type-F** (Russellian) monism, a non-physicalist option the corpus covers at concepts/russellian-monism. (d) L80's gloss, that zombies are possible yet consciousness "supervenes on the physical with metaphysical necessity even across possible worlds", is internally inconsistent: a possible zombie world is a failure of metaphysical supervenience. Fix: give the response map Chalmers's actual rows (A denies premise 1 by denying conceivability; B and C as he states them; F denies premise 3; Q rejects the framework), relabel or delete the invented Type-Q, and remove the inconsistent gloss. Use the verified quotations in the research note obsidian/research/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism-2026-10-02.md §Misassignment Register; quote nothing that is not there. Record in the changelog that the earlier stability notes were in error.

### ✓ 2026-10-02: Explanatory-gap hub runs a Type-C argument against physicalism generally (L135) — scope it, word-neutral
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/explanatory-gap.md
- **Notes**: L135 reads "If consciousness were identical to physical processes, we would expect the identity to be explanatorily satisfying once we had the facts—as with water and H₂O. The persistent dissatisfaction suggests the identity doesn't hold" and says "physicalists presuppose it can be closed; dualists deny it". Type-B physicalism (Loar, Papineau, Balog; Lycan, Tye, Carruthers as Chalmers classes them) denies that expectation: it holds the identity epistemically primitive and explains the residual gap by phenomenal concepts, so it does not presuppose closure. As written the paragraph is the Type-C reply presented against physicalism as a whole, and it contradicts concepts/neural-correlates-of-consciousness L142, which states the type-B position correctly. This is the hub page that should get the types right. Fix shape (from the research note obsidian/research/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism-2026-10-02.md §Misassignment Register): "physicalists presuppose" → "promissory (Type-C) physicalists presuppose" or equivalent, and scope the dissatisfaction argument to views that predict closure, funded by cutting words elsewhere in the paragraph. A piped link to concepts/type-a-type-b-and-type-c-physicalism on existing text costs zero words. Verify in both trees after sync.

### ✓ 2026-10-02: Filter-theory section of neural-correlates-of-consciousness: demote "support" to compatibility, replace the covert-consciousness figures with the canonical anchor, fix the covert, Santander, DMN and anaesthesia readings
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/neural-correlates-of-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-01, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Merged 2026-10-02 by /combine-outer-reviews. This task now also holds item (b) of the ChatGPT empirical-precision task, which edits the same bullet (L117), together with that task's driver-added Ledger field above. Both reviewers object to the lead-in. ChatGPT §7.3 says the article "leaves the heading 'Several findings support this reading'". Claude P10 says "DEMOTE-TO-COHERENCE-ONLY". L122's own concession ("cannot honestly be cited as independent confirmation") was checked on the live page. Change L115 to compatibility wording. (a) Covert-consciousness figures, L117. Both reviewers flag them. Check them against the canonical page [[covert-consciousness-and-cognitive-motor-dissociation#the-numbers-and-their-denominators]], not against either reviewer's numbers. That page gives: Kondziella 2016 = 14% of VS and 32% of MCS, "roughly 15%" of VS; Claassen 2019 = 16 of 104 (15%); Bodien 2024 = 60 of 241 (25%) without observable command-following, from a convenience sample of 353 at six centres. "Up to 25%" is Aubinet et al. 2025's broader umbrella figure, which pools complexity, passive EEG and command-following and is "not a CMD rate". So L117 has three faults. Its "up to 25% … (Kondziella et al., 2016)" misattributes the figure. Its "Meta-analyses suggest" does not fit that figure. Its "Claassen et al. (2019) and subsequent work report lower estimates (around 15%) under stricter command-following criteria" runs the wrong way (ChatGPT §4.4: "out of date"; Claude row 7: "the direction of the controversy backwards"). Following the Ledger, replace the restated figures with one clause and a piped link to that anchor. If a figure stays, use the canonical one-line statement, which ends "… the true figure is unknown". Never call these rates "established". Remove the Kondziella and Claassen references if the new clause no longer cites them. The filter-theory sibling's "Around 15% … (Claassen et al. 2019; Owen et al. 2006)" is Claude fix 16, a singleton, and is not part of this task. The falsifier task's note, which had called these rates "established at ~14–25%", was corrected on 2026-10-02 against the same anchor. (b) Delete "if awareness persists when its supposed neural basis fails to generate behavioural outputs, consciousness is not identical to those outputs". Both reviewers say it attacks no one. ChatGPT §4.4: "argues against a position few serious physicalists hold". Claude §2.2: "attacks a target nobody holds". Covert command-following is detected by preserved cortical activity. (c) Santander, L118. Both reviewers flag it. ChatGPT §4.5: "the inference points the wrong way". Claude row 12: "Co-optation." The article's own L122 already gives the production reading ("callosal integration as a generator needing only a thin posterior channel"). Move it out of the support list as a production-friendly channel-dependence case. ChatGPT adds that complete callosotomy produced disruption (PMC12582319); include that as one clause at most. (d) DMN, L119. Both reviewers attack the bullet's inference. ChatGPT §4.6 calls "Dissolves the Default Mode Network" "a journalistic metaphor" and says the datum "has little discriminatory value". Claude §2.2: "No production theory identifies the DMN as the generator of consciousness". Replace "dissolve" with the measured desynchronisation and reorganisation. Drop "If consciousness were generated by these neural patterns, disrupting them should diminish experience". Bring in [[filter-theory]]'s own concessions, "metabolically expensive" and "the metabolic data discriminates no better than the imaging"; both phrases are present on that page. (e) Anaesthesia, L120, plus L122's echo "near-total extinction". Here the reviewers disagree on the facts (synthesis Divergences). ChatGPT §4.7 cites Bajwa et al. 2025 (*Sci Rep*, doi:10.1038/s41598-025-12695-z) against "near-total extinction". Its Europe PMC abstract, checked 2026-10-02, reports that 24 of 52 attempted awakenings from deep propofol sedation produced reports of experience and 5 produced reports of none. It also found that PCIst and LZc did not differ between dreaming and non-dreaming periods. Claude alone (a singleton) proposes Sarasso et al. 2015 (*Curr Biol* 25(23):3099–3105, doi:10.1016/j.cub.2015.10.014) as the strongest production-side datum. Do not write "near-total extinction" as a general claim. If you add Sarasso, the same sentence must also carry Bajwa's null result within sedation. Bring in the calibration from [[anaesthesia-and-the-consciousness-interface]] L73 ("serves the filter reading no more than its rivals"; Xiong et al. 2024) by link, not restatement. (f) Predictive processing is a singleton (Claude §2.5, fix 7), but its absence is confirmed: 0 hits for "predictive" on the page. [[predictive-processing-and-dualism]] already covers Laukkonen, Friston & Chandaria 2025 (*Neurosci Biobehav Rev* 176:106296). Write a two-sentence link: that framework claims the psychedelic, meditative and cessation explananda under one mechanism, and filter theory currently offers no prediction that separates the two. ### ✓ 2026-10-02: Definition, lead, V4 and COGITATE precision in neural-correlates-of-consciousness: Koch et al. 2016 three-way taxonomy, Chalmers 2000 definition, variable achromatopsia, sourced Koch reading, preregistered outcomes
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/neural-correlates-of-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-01, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Merged 2026-10-02 by /combine-outer-reviews. This task now also holds three items from ChatGPT tasks that edited the same paragraphs: (a) V4 and (c) the Koch reading from the empirical-precision task, and (a) Naccache from the cross-review task. Every locus was re-grepped on 2026-10-02. (a) Definition, L55. Checked at the publisher on 2026-10-02 (nature.com, Key Points of Koch, Massimini, Boly & Tononi 2016, *Nat Rev Neurosci* 17(5):307–321, doi:10.1038/nrn.2016.22): "The neuronal correlates of consciousness (NCC) are the minimum neuronal mechanisms jointly sufficient for any one specific conscious experience. It is important to distinguish full NCC (the neural substrate supporting experience in general, irrespective of its specific content), content-specific NCC (…) and background conditions (factors that enable consciousness, but do not contribute directly to the content of experience — for example, arousal systems that ensure adequate excitability of the NCC)." So L55's "full NCC … involving brainstem and thalamic arousal systems" is the conflation both reviewers name. ChatGPT §2: arousal systems "are paradigmatic enabling background conditions". Claude row 5: "The article folds brainstem/thalamic arousal into 'full NCC'". Move arousal systems to background conditions. L55's quoted definition is spliced. Its "minimal … jointly sufficient … percept" matches neither the Key Points ("minimum … jointly sufficient … experience") nor the abstract ("minimum neural mechanisms sufficient for any one specific conscious percept"). Quote the Key Points verbatim and cite Koch et al. 2016 inline, which also uses the orphan reference 5. The collecting pass said "jointly sufficient" is only Koch's 2004 wording. That was wrong: the 2016 Key Points contain it. (b) Chalmers 2000 (reference 1) is never used in the body. Checked at consc.net/papers/ncc2.html: his first pass is the ASSC formulation "correlates directly". His §4 "Overall definition" reads "An NCC is a minimal neural system N such that there is a mapping from states of N to states of consciousness, where a given state of N is sufficient, under conditions C, for the corresponding state of consciousness". He also separates "(2) What do we mean by 'neural correlate of consciousness'?" from "(5) Is consciousness reducible to its neural correlate(s)?" That separation is the article's best support for neutrality, so add it in one sentence (a natural place is the "Correlation Is Not Identity" opening). The reviewers frame this differently. ChatGPT calls minimal sufficiency "one developed proposal"; Claude calls it Chalmers's definition. Both are partly right, so call it his "overall" definition. Trap: Chalmers's "background state of consciousness" (being awake, dreaming, under hypnosis) is itself something an NCC can be found for. It is not Koch et al.'s "background conditions", which are enablers outside the NCC. Do not merge the two. Verify the page range (17–40, from Claude row 1) before adding it. (c) Lead, L51. Both reviewers flag it. ChatGPT §4.3: "an influential hypothesis, not an established general finding". Claude P2: "Present it as an IIT-aligned, contested hypothesis". Koch et al. 2016's abstract makes the posterior-hot-zone claim, so attribute it to them and mark it as contested; L59 already says "the interpretation remains contested". L59's no-report sentence is already hedged ("suggest", "may"). Both reviewers want it sourced or marked as contested, but a citation (Tsuchiya et al. 2015, unverified) is optional because of the budget. (d) V4, L59. Both reviewers flag it, and it checks out. The Europe PMC abstract of Bouvier & Engel 2006 (*Cerebral Cortex* 16(2):183–191, doi:10.1093/cercor/bhi096) covers 92 cases and says "The severity of color vision deficits of the cases varied greatly" and that the deficits are "often incomplete and never restricted to color vision". Replace "damage to V4 causes complete loss of color experience (achromatopsia)" with severe but variable cerebral achromatopsia after ventral occipital lesions, and cite the paper. Both reviewers also add "bilateral", but the abstract does not say it, so do not add "bilateral" without a source. The same overclaim recurs at L156, which belongs to the illusionism/Dualism task; that task aligns to the wording installed here. (e) COGITATE, L63. Both reviewers say the paragraph neither reports the preregistered outcomes nor gives a source for Koch's reading. Replace "Neither theory was fully vindicated; the headline result was that the findings were decidedly mixed…" and the closing hedge sentence with the abstract's outcomes, which the Claude collecting pass checked at Europe PMC (doi:10.1038/s41586-025-08888-1): content decoded in "visual, ventrotemporal and inferior frontal cortex"; "a lack of sustained synchronization within the posterior cortex" against IIT; and "the general lack of ignition at stimulus offset and limited representation of certain conscious dimensions in the prefrontal cortex" against GNWT. Koch's reading has a source. A Reuters piece by Will Dunham, "Scientists explore where consciousness arises in the brain" (the syndicated kfgo.com copy fetched 2026-10-02 is dated 1 May 2025; confirm the Reuters date), quotes him: "Here, the evidence is decidedly in favor of the posterior cortex. Either information pertaining to the conscious experience couldn't be found in the front or it was far weaker than in the back." Cite it as a press interview; the research note's L99 carries the quotation without a URL. Replace "contest on methodological grounds" with one clause naming the substantive GNWT reply (Naccache et al. 2025, *Neurosci Conscious* niaf037: onset ignition at 200–800 ms validated; clearly visible stimuli could not test the conscious/nonconscious contrast; offset ignition was not a core prediction). Link it with a piped link to [[global-workspace-theory]], which already summarises the reply, and add no reference entry. Do not build ChatGPT's prediction table (improvement 5), which would cost about 150 words. Claude's IIT-pseudoscience letter is a singleton, and [[integrated-information-theory]] already covers it, so a link at most. (f) Reciprocal: `obsidian/concepts/visual-consciousness.md` L87, "found consciousness-related activity concentrated in posterior regions rather than prefrontal cortex", overstates the same abstract. Align it, measuring that host first. **Length budget: net ≤ +20 words on the NCC page.** Fund it with these named cuts: the L109 hippocampal-binding sentence (30 words), which both reviewers flag as unsourced (ChatGPT §4.9; Claude §2.1, unreferenced-claims row). It is not guarded: the 2026-05-13 deep review already trimmed its restatement. When cutting it, shorten the [[binding-problem]] gloss in Further Reading. Also drop the orphan Dennett reference (reference 2, never cited in the body) unless the report-dependence section cites it, and drop L51's rhetorical question "When you see red, what patterns of brain activity correspond to that experience?". Additions are the definition, the Chalmers sentence, and two reference lines (Bouvier & Engel 2006; Dunham/Reuters 2025). Re-measure before and after. Bump `ai_modified` on each host; changelog before sync; sync; Hugo grep.

### ✓ 2026-10-02: Illusionism and Dualism sections of neural-correlates-of-consciousness: replace the bare seeming-regress with Frankish's reply, add the Type-B reply to the opacity inference, fix the Tallis reference
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/neural-correlates-of-consciousness.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-10-01, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. Merged 2026-10-02 by /combine-outer-reviews. This task now also holds item (d) of the ChatGPT empirical-precision task (the Tallis reference for the L126 sentence rewritten here) and item (d) of the tenet-verb task (the L156 verb). (a) L126 regress. Both reviewers flag it. ChatGPT §6: "the article cannot refute it simply by reinstating phenomenal seeming as a premise". Claude row 3: "Frankish's quasi-phenomenal-property reply to the 'seeming must be phenomenal' regress is not addressed." Checked live: [[illusionism]] L91 says "But the bare regress assumes 'seeming' is itself phenomenal—precisely what illusionists deny." The survey has to match its specialist child page. State Frankish's functional-seeming reply, which makes reference 3 cited. Then give the stronger objection: whether metarepresentation can explain the specific structure and qualitative character of introspection (ChatGPT §6). (b) Tallis. Both reviewers object to the reference and to Tallis as the only voice against illusionism. The PDCnet record, checked 2026-10-02, is "Tallis in Wonderland: The Illusion of Illusionism", *Philosophy Now* 161, 58–59 (2024). Fix the reference line and condense the Tallis sentence. ChatGPT §6 adds that L128's "Both camps make the same empirical predictions" is too quick. That is a singleton, so this item is optional. (c) L154–156, Dualism. Both reviewers flag it, and grep finds 0 hits for "Type-B" and for "phenomenal concept". ChatGPT §5.2: "It begs the question against the phenomenal-concept strategy". Claude §2.3 ¶3 names "the Type-B physicalist / phenomenal-concept-strategy prediction that the gap *will persist* under physicalism". Replace "If consciousness were identical to neural activity, we would expect the identity to eventually become intelligible", the sentence both reviewers target, with the Type-B reply, linked to [[phenomenal-concepts-strategy]]. Note that Levine 1983 drew an epistemic, not an ontological, conclusion; that makes reference 8 cited (ChatGPT §5.2; Claude row 8). Downgrade "This persistent opacity supports Dualism" to wording such as "the Map reads … as consonant with Dualism" (Claude fix 12; the tenet-verb task leaves this to you). L154 says "both physicalism and interactionism predict exactly the correlations NCC research discovers". Both reviewers reject this (ChatGPT §1: "Generic interactionism does not predict 'exactly the correlations'"; Claude §2.3 ¶2: "false on any likelihood-sensitive reading"). Change "predict exactly" to accommodate-wording that matches the L81 clause from the IEP-quotation P1 task. L156's "We can predict that V4 damage eliminates color experience" should match the variable-deficit wording from the definition/V4 task (Bouvier & Engel 2006: deficits "often incomplete"). Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [[writing-style|the writing-style guide]]'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. **Length budget: net ≤ +10 words**, with no new references. Fund it by condensing the regress fork (about 45 words) and the Tallis sentence, and by deleting the 16-word "we would expect … intelligible" sentence. Re-measure before and after. Bump `ai_modified`; changelog before sync; sync; Hugo grep.

The IEP entry "Dualism and Mind" (Scott Calef), §7.d "The Correlation and Dependence Arguments", reads verbatim: "As for correlation, interactionism actually predicts that mental events are caused by brain events and vice versa, so the fact that perceptions are correlated with activity in the visual cortex does not support materialism over this form of dualism." "Dualism and Mind." *Internet Encyclopedia of Philosophy*, §7.d, https://iep.utm.edu/dualism-and-mind/). Keep the SEP reference only if (c) cites it. You may soften the L79 heading to "Interactionism Expects Correlations". The matching L154 wording belongs to the illusionism/Dualism task; keep the two consistent. (c) This is also convergent: grep finds 0 hits for "exclusion", "Papineau" and "Kim", and "closure" occurs only in falsifier 4, where it means closing the gap. It reports that as an argument and then gives Mills's reply ("Mills says that this argument is invalid"). So attribute it as the closure objection the SEP discusses, never as the SEP's verdict. Use one clause linking [[causal-closure]] and no Papineau/Kim prose. Do not treat the research note or `pessimistic-2026-03-19-night` L49 as confirmation. **Length budget: net ≤ +15 words.** The page is at 3,419 by `tools.curate.length.analyze_length` at 2026-10-02 01:36Z, and the concepts hard gate is 3,500 `>=`. ### ✓ 2026-10-01: Cross-review thought insertion B: relabel the kind-claim falsifier as conceptual on three pages, net-negative
- **Type**: cross-review

### ✓ 2026-10-01: Write article on the Revelation thesis — what experience reveals about phenomenal properties and what the Map may not assume
- **Type**: expand-topic
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/concepts/revelation-thesis.md
- **Notes**: Slug `revelation-thesis` (free 2026-10-01 01:35Z in obsidian/ and archive/). Concepts 338/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files` at 01:25Z (re-measure at pick time). Build the note's Angle 1 (§Potential Article Angles L207–213): "The Revelation Thesis: What Experience Reveals and What the Map May Not Assume" — (i) Johnston's colour constraint and Byrne & Hilbert 2007's decomposition (colour-Revelation vs "Revelation-about-the-mind" are TWO formally parallel theses; the Map's residue is the bridge: relocating colour into experience turns the one into the other); (ii) the phenomenal version — Lewis 1995's Identification Thesis, Stoljar's denial, the Damnjanovic 2012 (*Dialectica* 66(1): 69–91, NOT Synthese) / Trogdon 2017 partial-vs-full result; (iii) Goff's Russellian use and the both-ways turn (Majeed 2017; Lin 2025); (iv) LEAD WITH THE MAP'S REASON FOR DENYING STRONG REVELATION (LLMs know Lewis and Goff; they do not know this): the Map needs only WEAK Revelation (existence + partial nature — Goff's "translucent", Trogdon's partial characterisation, Lewis's "harmless look-alikes"), which is compatible with physicalism, tier COMPATIBLE, no discriminator; it must NOT assume STRONG Revelation (full essence — Lewis's Identification, Goff's transparency) for three reasons the note documents: Lewis's no-hidden-grain corollary contradicts `self-opacity` / `introspection`; Goff 2018 (*Topoi*) — "Revelation is a source of pressure in direction of epiphenomenalism, as introspection does not seem to reveal our conscious states as being essentially causal" — would make the Tenet 2–3 interface power inessential (`positions/quantum-interface` L145: "only indirect traces"); and strong Revelation is the Russellian rival's premise. Net: the argument from revelation is NOT available to the Map for Tenet 1; the PCS denies strong Revelation and `tenets.md` L103 / `phenomenal-concepts-strategy` L141 commit the Map only to weak. (v) terminology fix: 21 live pages use "phenomenal transparency" in Metzinger's sense — OPPOSITE polarity to Goff's — the page must disambiguate explicitly. Co-optation firewall for Lewis, Stoljar, Papineau (each a physicalist; stance sentence each). **Note's Gaps propagate**: Johnston 1992 raw text NOT retrieved — the constraint's wording rests on three agreeing secondary reproductions (SEP §1.4, Byrne & Hilbert 2007 at reprint p. 138, a slide summary) — quote it only as reproduced by SEP/Byrne & Hilbert; do NOT write "coined" (three sources say Johnston *named* it — the named-idea-cited-to-its-populariser trap); Trogdon 2017, Goff 2011/2015/2017, Damnjanovic full texts unread (abstract or metadata only — mark them); full-text grep-verified: Byrne & Hilbert 2007, Lewis 1995, Liu 2019, Liu 2021, Cortesi 2023, Chalmers 2003, SEP. Quote only spans the note marks grep-verified. Reciprocals (measure first): `colour-ontology-and-the-secondary-quality-residue` L52/L66 (~2888/4000 — one sentence), `russellian-monism` and `russellian-monism-versus-bi-aspectual-dualism` (topics/), `intrinsic-nature` (2422/3500 — one sentence), `phenomenal-concepts-strategy` (3477/3500 — piped only), `intrinsic-nature-void`, `meta-problem-of-consciousness` — see the note's "Articles That Should Later Cite It" L215–229 with measured headroom. Target 2,200–2,800 body words; concepts hard gate 3,500. `topics:` bare slugs; ai_modified from the real clock; changelog before sync; sync; validate. ### ✓ 2026-10-01: Write article on depersonalisation as the clinical datum for separable for-me-ness
- **Type**: expand-topic

- **Notes**: Slug `depersonalisation` (free 2026-10-01 00:16Z). Concepts 337/360 by `tools.evolution.state.count_section_files` at 23:50Z (re-measure at pick time). Note is 9,827 words with 42 citations — build the note's recommended angle (§Potential Article Angles, L276–296): a HOME PAGE for the Map's most-cited clinical datum, so that the six host sections (clinical-dissociation-as-systematic-evidence L84, clinical-phenomenology-and-altered-experience L105, mine-ness L70, cotard-delusion L49, phenomenal-quality-void L94, necessary-opacity L80) can point at one careful statement instead of restating it. LEAD WITH THE TWO-TIER VERDICT (as cotard-delusion does): architecture claim — a separable for-me-ness component — defensible at level 1 and CONTESTED at level 2 (Zahavi & Kriegel 2016 universalism, for-me-ness "not … a detachable self quale"; Knappik 2022 localism; neither names depersonalisation — say so); significance claim — whether separability marks anything non-physical — NULL: Billon (2016, 2017, 2023 OUP chapter, 2024 *Metaphilosophy* 55(1)) grants the strongest architecture reading and closes significance himself (mineness explicable "in purely neurophysiological terms" — source-role: supporting at levels 1–2, recruited-by at level 4); the affective/inhibitory model (Sierra & Berrios 1998; Sierra & David 2011; Gerrans 2019) and the active-inference self-model reading (Seth et al. 2012; Ciaunica et al. 2022; Deane et al. 2020) are fully specified physicalist rivals. Tenet 1: COMPATIBLE only, the tier mine-ness and cotard already hold. Diagnostic facts: insight retention is DSM-5 Criterion B ("reality testing remains intact"); the "as if" is CDS/Billon wording, NOT DSM text — do not attribute it to the DSM. **Note's Gaps propagate**: Sierra 2009 and Simeon & Abugel 2006 unread (metadata only); Sass et al. 2013 body unread (four authors, not two — do not write "Sass & Pienkos"); Gerrans 2019/2023/2024 unread; abstract-only sources (Sierra & Berrios 1998/2000, Sierra et al. 2005, Sierra & David 2011, Hunter et al. 2004 — author order Hunter, Sierra, David per PubMed, Medford 2012, Davies & Coltheart 2022) may not be quoted beyond their abstracts; Billon quotes came via Wayback copies and carry no page numbers — cite without pages. Year resolutions: Billon 2023 and 2024 are two different works; Ciaunica, Charlton & Farmer is *Phenomenology and the Cognitive Sciences* 20(1): 1–19, cite as 2021. Quote only spans the note marks grep-verified. Reciprocals: the six hosts are over or near their gates (mine-ness 3496/3500 — piped only; necessary-opacity ~2643/3000; phenomenal-quality-void and clinical-phenomenology measure first) — install `[[depersonalisation|existing words]]` piped links where no headroom exists, one sentence where there is; do NOT rewrite the hosts' sections (that is the sibling refine task on their defects). ### ✓ 2026-09-30: `positions/ai-substrate-verdicts` P-AS1 scores the analog class on continuity, specificity and granularity but inherits the gate class's locality pass by silence, although its continuity partial-pass is bought with "spatially distributed" selection
- **Type**: positions-evolve
- **File**: /home/andy/unfin/unfinishablemap/obsidian/positions/ai-substrate-verdicts.md
- **Notes**: Grep-verified 2026-09-30. L41: "Gate-based processors pass directness in the raw sense and plausibly pass locality" (reason at `topics/quantum-hardware-and-the-ai-consciousness-coupling` L54: "Qubits are individually addressable, physically localised structures"); L41: "The analog class (annealers, analog simulators) softens continuity to a partial pass, since selection there is temporally extended and spatially distributed". The locality requirement reads "Interface would need to operate in specific structures, not diffusely" (`topics/quantum-randomness-channel-llm-consciousness` L80). Spatially distributed heterogeneous freeze-out is the opposite property, and the entry never re-scores locality for the class; the apex's "scores at two requirements of five" (`apex/assessing-ai-consciousness-under-the-map` L135) assumes the inheritance. Fix: score locality for the analog class explicitly in one clause. If per-qubit freeze-out counts as localised (each qubit stabilises individually — Pelofske, Hahn & Djidjev 2019, already cited by the topic at L63), say so; if not, record the loss and re-derive the apex's requirement count. No calibration change expected either way. Length: the register tolerates a dated `Updated` line; keep the addition to two sentences. Sync obsidian → hugo and grep-verify both trees. See [[reviews/pessimistic-2026-09-30-ai-substrate-verdicts]] Issue 5.

### ✓ 2026-09-30: `positions/ai-substrate-verdicts` P-AS1 L41 states the gate-class continuity failure as a single terminal collapse in the sentence that invokes syndrome extraction, and its specificity failure on a fixed measurement basis — a context-selection requirement Tenet 3 disclaims

- **Notes**: Grep-verified 2026-09-30. L41: "they fail continuity, specificity, and granularity, because the machine's value lies in evolving superpositions unitarily until a terminal readout, quantum error correction actively isolates the logical state from external influence — precisely the influence an interface would need — and syndrome extraction projects onto a fixed code basis rather than hosting open selection." Syndrome extraction IS repeated mid-evolution projective measurement (the cited Google 2024 result reports logical error per cycle); the dependent topic article distinguishes annealers by protection arriving "never as a mid-evolution measurement" (`topics/quantum-hardware-and-the-ai-consciousness-coupling` L41) and words its own continuity cell as "no ongoing stream of collapse events at decision-relevant points" (L55) — the register dropped "decision-relevant" (L41 has zero occurrences; the file's only one is at L44). Second defect, same sentence: `tenets.md` L107 commits the Map to "influence over *which outcome* becomes actual, not merely over which measurement the brain performs", and under P-Q1 (`positions/quantum-interface` L55, "selection acts on already-decohered branch-outcomes") the biological basis is the einselected pointer basis — fixed, like the code basis. Basis-fixedness therefore does not separate the machine from the brain, and "rather than hosting open selection" conflates a fixed basis with a closed outcome (syndrome outcomes are open Born events). Fix, argument-only, no calibration change: (a) restate continuity in the annealer's currency the topic article already supplies (L65 "continuity of dynamics is not continuity of selection") — the QEC'd machine hosts an ongoing stream of open events whose selected outcomes the decoder is built to erase — and restore "at decision-relevant points"; (b) replace the fixed-basis clause with that erasure argument; (c) in *Would shift if* (L44) delete "a measurement basis not fixed in advance" or re-key it to "collapse events whose selected outcome is consumed rather than corrected"; (d) optional same-pass wording: credit the directness pass to the un-laundered readout event rather than to "the superposition is genuine and live" (L41), since P-Q1 does not use maintained coherence. If the Map DOES mean to require basis-openness of an artificial host, say so and reconcile with Tenet 3 explicitly — that would be a new commitment, flag it rather than bury it. Do NOT touch the third bucket, the B/D split, or the 2026-09-24 note. Add a dated `Updated 2026-09-30` line and bump *Last reviewed* (L45, still 2026-08-20). Sync obsidian → hugo and grep-verify both trees. See [[reviews/pessimistic-2026-09-30-ai-substrate-verdicts]] Issues 1–2.

### ✓ 2026-09-30: `concepts/galilean-exclusion` — Husserl, Whitehead and the *Blind Spot* authors are conscripted for a conclusion they reject, and the tenet section states "it is expected" with no discriminator and no objections section
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/galilean-exclusion.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-30, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, chatgpt]. Both reviewers find the cited phenomenologists conscripted for a conclusion they reject and the tenet section upgrading compatibility into evidence — Claude: "The diagnosis does not discriminate between rival views, yet the page uses it as if it supported dualism"; ChatGPT: "the tenet is allowed to settle the interpretation of the history: once irreducibility is assumed, the methodological omission becomes evidence of a structural limit". Both make the functionalism category error (a metaphysical thesis, not a data policy — ChatGPT: "A functionalist does not normally say: 'We cannot reach experience, so let us study reports instead'"), the Occam point (identity theorists identify rather than omit), and the missing-objections point. Claude-review detail follows; run after the sibling P1 on the Galileo reading. Verified during processing: Whitehead explicitly *rejects* the "psychic additions" theory (Gutenberg #18835 — "all our sense-perceptions are in the same boat"), so the bifurcation critique targets exactly the placing of qualities in a mind that dualism formalises; the *Blind Spot* authors place "naturalistic dualism, substance dualism, or panpsychism" and illusionism "within the ambit of the Blind Spot" (p. 196 per Closer To Truth's landscape entry — the reviewer's p. 192 quotation was **not** confirmed there; verify at the book before quoting either); Husserl's cure is transcendental phenomenology and his *Crisis* §9 argument concerns the life-world as meaning-fundament, not the neural explanatory gap; Goff's *Galileo's Error* (2019) runs the identical diagnosis to panpsychism and is already cited in `concepts/panpsychism` but not here. The writing-style guide's co-optation firewall (§"Don't Conscript Committed Physicalists") requires a one-line statement of each cited author's actual framework in any tenet-load-bearing passage: add them for Husserl, Whitehead, Thompson/Frank/Gleiser and Chalmers (a naturalistic *property* dualist who leans epiphenomenalist in TCM). Tenet section, per `project/evidential-status-discipline` §compatibility-vs-support (the independent-discriminator requirement): **Dualism** — "the failure of that method to explain subjectivity ... is expected" is expected equally on type-B physicalism, Russellian monism, illusionism and Blind-Spot non-dualism, so cap it at *compatible / non-discriminating* and say which rivals predict the same datum; **Bidirectional Interaction** — "A science that excludes consciousness from its descriptions will miss any causal contributions consciousness makes" conflicts with the Map's own Born-preserving mechanism (the contribution is invisible to aggregate tests by *design*, not because of the Galilean exclusion) — reconcile and link the born-rule pages; **Occam** — "simplicity through omission" hits only a naive argument; identity theorists *identify* experience rather than omit it, and the live materialist argument is causal completeness (see `concepts/parsimony-epistemology`, `concepts/type-identity-theory`). Add a "What would challenge this view" / objections section covering Dennett (heterophenomenology is a data policy, not a denial), Churchland, Frankish, Strawson, Goff, Russellian monism (Newman's problem points toward it — say so, and point at `[[ontic-structural-realism#newman]]`, which already concedes it cuts both ways, and at that page's L59 admission that the "strongest reading" is the Map's own join), and predictive processing: link `[[predictive-processing]]` and engage Laukkonen, Friston & Chandaria (2025), *Neurosci. Biobehav. Rev.* 176:106296 (Crossref-verified; already the named default rival in evidential-status-discipline) — a live attempt to make phenomenal structure the *target* of a Galilean-descended formal science, which is the case the sieve claim says cannot occur. Fix the category error in "functionalism admits experience only as third-person data": functionalism is a metaphysical thesis, the data policy is separate, and the article itself concedes two sections later that neurophenomenology "break[s] from the framework". Concede that psychophysics and colorimetry made secondary qualities scientific targets (relational structure is tractable) and restrict the residue to intrinsic quality. Apply the direct-refutation discipline. Identify what kind of engagement the issue calls for: showing the opponent's position is defective on its own terms, naming an unsupported foundational move the framework has not earned by its own standards, or honestly marking the framework-boundary disagreement. Apply the corresponding reply mode in **natural journal-quality prose** — see [[writing-style|the writing-style guide]]'s "Engaging Opponents in Journal-Quality Prose" section. **Do not expose mode labels in the article body.** The classification is editor-internal; it belongs in the refine-draft / deep-review changelog entry, not in the article. If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, state in natural language that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. Folded from the ChatGPT review (adjudicated in the synthesis): (i) objections section — add identity theory and the phenomenal-concept strategy ("one property under distinct concepts"; "an epistemic or conceptual gap without an ontological gap") and representational or relational theories of colour as the direct answers to the move from descriptive difference to ontological difference; the page currently offers only "intrinsic content excluded by physics" or "abolished"; (ii) tenet section — ChatGPT's calibrated formulations are usable nearly verbatim: Dualism, "The genealogy is compatible with dualism but does not independently establish it"; Bidirectional, "This is what follows from Tenet 3; the Galilean history supplies no independent evidence for downward causation"; Occam, "The genealogy blocks a particular parsimony argument ... Whether a materialist account actually omits phenomenal facts remains the substantive dispute"; (iii) use *The Blind Spot* symmetrically — the authors distinguish scientific abstraction from the metaphysics reified onto it (Thompson, Love & Philosophy interview, fetched: "nothing in the project of science itself ... has to be pinned to ... a metaphysical hierarchy") and their constructive programme presupposes that science can be reformed, so the stance sentence must also concede the revisability objection (a sieve has a fixed mesh; science does not); (iv) OSR — ChatGPT rates the paragraph "one of the strongest passages" after the 09-05 repair and asks only for two sentences (ordinary OSR is largely silent on consciousness and phenomenal structuralism supplies the extension; Newman is pressure, not decisive refutation) — that is the shape the Claude ask reduces to, since the OSR page already carries the concessions; (v) neurophenomenology — make `topics/methodology-of-consciousness-research` (L108 "a real advance") the authority and point the target's rigour sentence at it. Disputed and excluded from the convergence: Claude's *Blind Spot* p.192 quotation (not carried by the cited source; the stance finding stands on p.196 and the Thompson interview).

### ✓ 2026-09-30: `concepts/galilean-exclusion` — the "Not a Discovery but a Decision" thesis is reversed against *Il Saggiatore* §48 (verified verbatim: "puri nomi", "levate ed annichilate", "primi e reali accidenti", "corpo sensitivo")
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/concepts/galilean-exclusion.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-30, 2/3 reviewers): [claude, chatgpt]. Both reviewers reverse the "decision, not discovery" reading and both were verified at source during processing (Claude against *Il Saggiatore* §48 verbatim; ChatGPT against SEP "Primary and Secondary Qualities", fetched) — Claude: "Galileo makes an ontological claim ('puri nomi,' 'annichilate,' contrast with 'primi e reali accidenti'); the article calls it a methodological decision"; ChatGPT: "His argument is explicitly about which attributes genuinely belong to external bodies, not only about which variables it is useful for physicists to model." Both keep the "not a discovery" half and the undercutting argument (physics' success without phenomenal vocabulary does not show phenomenal reality reducible). Both also flag "structurally inevitable" (Claude: "historical over-determination"; ChatGPT: "Do not say Galileo made the hard problem logically or historically inevitable"), "because Galileo made them so" (both: Chalmers's argument is modal and conceptual, not genealogical — ChatGPT: "the article reverses the order of support"), and the sieve (Claude: factually inverted; ChatGPT: "sound only as a conditional or undercutting argument" whose second premise — phenomenal character is not structure or function — is the disputed proposition the history cannot establish). Claude-review detail follows. The primary text was checked at it.wikisource.org/wiki/Il_Saggiatore/48 during processing: Galileo calls tastes, odours and colours "puri nomi" that are "levate ed annichilate" once the animal is removed, contrasts them with "primi e reali accidenti", and locates them in the "corpo sensitivo" — ontological vocabulary, tentatively voiced ("vo io pensando"), and a *body*, not Drake's "consciousness". The article's slogan gets "not a discovery" right and "decision" wrong; the defensible thesis is *a speculative atomist posit warranted by the mathematical programme's success, not by evidence about qualities*. Loci to fix, all in the live file: (1) lead — "did not merely organise phenomena. It defined what would count as scientifically real" and "made the hard problem ... structurally inevitable" (also the Further Reading blurb for hard-problem-of-consciousness: "the problem the exclusion made structurally inevitable") → "shaped the modern form of"; (2) section title "Not a Discovery but a Decision" and its opening "He *decided* to exclude them"; (3) internal contradiction — "What began as a methodological convenience in Galileo became, through Descartes, a metaphysical framework" versus the OSR paragraph's "he *relocates* the residue" (relocating is an ontological act; keep the latter); (4) the Boyle/Locke parenthetical — Galileo already writes "primi e reali accidenti", Boyle introduced the English terms before Locke; (5) the Descartes paragraph makes Cartesian dualism derivative of Galileo — it has independent roots; (6) "They are different in kind because Galileo made them so" — delete; Chalmers's gap is conceptual/modal, not genealogical; (7) the sieve analogy is factually inverted — sieve analysis is the standard method for measuring grain-size distribution, so a filter's selectivity is exactly what makes it an instrument for the thing filtered; delete or replace with an image where exclusion-from-description is visibly not exclusion-from-study; (8) "Qualia ... are precisely the secondary qualities Galileo excluded" equivocates between object-side qualities (Galileo/Locke) and properties of experience — separate them; (9) Thompson (2007) is cited for "modern science inherited from Galileo the assumption that mathematical-physical description exhausts what is objectively real" — the reviewer could not find this in *Mind in Life* and says it is *Blind Spot* (2024) wording: **verify at source before re-attributing** (unverified during processing); (10) add the Galileo page (Drake p. 274, *Opere* VI 347–350) with the translation caveat, and the Further Reading blurb for philosophy-of-science-under-dualism ("methodological choice rather than a metaphysical discovery") — that page itself carries no Galilean text, so only the blurb changes. Keep the symmetric anti-materialist point (success of physics without qualities is no evidence against them) and add its mirror (the method's failure to capture qualities is no evidence *for* their non-physicality). Length measured by `analyze_length`: 2010 words against concepts soft 2500 / hard 3500 — ~1490 words of headroom, so this and the sibling P1 below fit without trimming. Do this task first; the sibling depends on the corrected reading. Prior internal passes (deep-review 2026-06-01, 2026-09-05; tenet-checks 2026-06-19b, 2026-09-06, 2026-09-23) all passed this page without testing the reading against the primary text. Folded from the ChatGPT review (adjudicated in the synthesis): (11) pinpoints — cite *Crisis* §9h–i, *The Concept of Nature* Ch. II ("Theories of the Bifurcation of Nature"), and either attach *Science and the Modern World* (currently unpinned at L64 and the reference list) to a specific chapter — the "fallacy of misplaced concreteness" — or drop it from this short concept page; (12) soften "made modern science possible" using SEP "Galileo Galilei" (rev. 2025-12-17, fetched during processing): the passage was "incidental to his own program", so label the Husserl–Whitehead genealogy as an interpretive reconstruction ("a founding move in one influential reconstruction of modern science", not "the foundational methodological move"); (13) the lead's "removed subjective experience from its domain of inquiry" → "excluded sensible qualities from the explanatory primitives of mathematical physics" — Galileo relocated them into the sentient body and proposed a causal programme for them (SEP, citing Piccolino & Wade 2008), which is why the OSR paragraph's "relocates" is the wording to keep in item (3); (14) when replacing the sieve, prefer ChatGPT's explicit conditional over a new image — Galilean explanation is structural-functional; phenomenal character is not exhausted by structure and function (contested: link the pages that defend it); therefore that explanatory form is incomplete — and add the symmetric statement that the genealogy blocks the inference from methodological success to ontological completeness while not establishing that no expanded science could explain experience. Recorded divergence, not to resolve here: ChatGPT endorses the Boyle/Locke parenthetical as correct where Claude calls it half-right — the narrow fix in (4) satisfies both.

### ✓ 2026-09-29: Update anaesthesia-and-the-consciousness-interface citations — 3 2020s papers missing
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/anaesthesia-and-the-consciousness-interface.md
- **Notes**: From literature-drift audit 2026-09-29. Article's empirical citations have median year 2021 (25 empirical references, Meyer 1899 / James 1898 excluded), which is 5 years behind its last-modified date (2026-08-22) — at the lag threshold, not over it; the drift signal is raised on the missing-citation check. The 2024–2026 anaesthesia literature has materially advanced on exactly the article's evidential frame (which cortical/hippocampal processing survives loss of consciousness, and how to measure conscious state behaviour-independently), and the article does not engage the following papers, each topically appropriate to its central claims:
  1. Katlowitz et al. 2026 — *Plasticity and language in the anaesthetized human hippocampus*, Nature 654(8119), 714–723, doi:10.1038/s41586-026-10448-0. Neuropixels single-unit recordings in anaesthetised epilepsy-surgery patients: hippocampal neurons and LFPs retained oddball discrimination that *grew* over ~10 min (representational plasticity), and carried semantic and grammatical information about natural speech, predicting semantic features of upcoming words. Bears directly on §Memory Encoding (Kallionpää 2018 is the current anchor for sub-threshold processing) and on §The Bootstrapping Problem's premise that "even under deep anaesthesia some neural processing continues" — this is the strongest human single-neuron evidence for that premise and it is uncited corpus-wide (0 Map files name Katlowitz).
  2. Xiong et al. 2024 — *Propofol-mediated loss of consciousness disrupts predictive routing and local field phase modulation of neural activity*, PNAS 121(42), e2315160121, doi:10.1073/pnas.2315160121 (Xiong, Donoghue, Lundqvist, Mahnke, Major, Brown, Miller, Bastos). Intracranial recordings in macaques across propofol LOC: alpha/beta feedback that inhibits predictable input in the awake state is abolished, sensory cortex becomes *disinhibited* (enhanced gamma and late spiking to oddballs), while frontal–sensory alpha/beta coherence and higher-order prediction-error decodability collapse. Bears on §Phenomenal Presence (propofol's local slow wave, Sarasso 2015) and §Content Without Access: propofol severs top-down/feedback routing rather than sensory processing, which is the disconnection-not-extinction framing stated at the molecular-circuit level. Uncited corpus-wide (0 Map files name Xiong or "predictive routing"). Note the predictive-processing seam: `topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism` should be checked for a reciprocal once integrated.
  3. Jiang & Sleigh 2024 — *Consciousness and General Anesthesia: Challenges for Measuring the Depth of Anesthesia*, Anesthesiology 140(2), 313–328, doi:10.1097/ALN.0000000000004830. Review of the connected/disconnected distinction and of behaviour-independent measures of subjective experience under anaesthesia. Bears on §Graded Consciousness and Filter Theory Predictions (PCI paragraph) and complements the Montupil 2023 / Bonhomme 2019 anchors with a measurement-side statement of the same disconnection framing. (Full text paywalled; metadata verified via Crossref.) Sleigh appears elsewhere in the Map only in unrelated contexts.
  4. Optional, frame-conditional: Khan et al. 2024 — *Microtubule-Stabilizer Epothilone B Delays Anesthetic-Induced Unconsciousness in Rats*, eNeuro 11(8), ENEURO.0291-24.2024, doi:10.1523/ENEURO.0291-24.2024. Epothilone B delayed isoflurane LORR by ~69 s (Cohen's d 1.9) in male rats. The article's Minimal Quantum Interaction paragraph deliberately declines to recruit the anaesthetic-microtubule line (it cites Wiest 2025, which reviews this study) and defers to `topics/quantum-biology-and-neural-consciousness`, where Khan 2024 is already cited along with ~30 other Map pages. Do NOT integrate it as support; at most name the primary study beside Wiest 2025 in that paragraph so the demotion cites the evidence it is demoting.
  Action: integrate items 1–3 into the article's evidence sections; item 1 should sharpen the Bootstrapping Problem's residual-processing premise and item 2 should sharpen the propofol/ketamine contrast (feedback-routing loss vs access loss) — if the 2020s literature's dominant framing has shifted toward predictive-routing / feedback-disruption accounts of propofol LOC, say so and let it constrain the filter reading rather than restating it; preserve the article's existing voice and its underdetermination markers ("suggestive rather than vindicating", "convergence of vocabulary"). Length: the article is 4103 words including reference apparatus, already above the topics/ 4000 hard-warning line, so integrate length-neutrally (trim or merge, do not append). **Important**: do NOT inflate the article's evidential calibration on the basis of more citations — see [[project/evidential-status-discipline]]. Items 1–2 are physicalist-neutral findings: they support a structured, partly-preserved substrate under anaesthesia, which every modular production model also predicts; the article must keep saying so (its §What Anaesthesia Cannot Tell Us already does). If a new citation supports a rival framing, acknowledge it. Sync to Hugo after editing.

### ✓ 2026-09-29: Constitutive exclusion — delayed-choice history wrong twice over, Kant opening conflates intuition-forms with categories, citation locators and two hygiene fixes
- **Type**: refine-draft

- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-29, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. **Upgraded P2→P1 by /combine-outer-reviews: three of the four items were raised by both legs**, which the Claude processing pass missed (it listed them as unique to its review). ChatGPT 5.6 Pro on Jacques et al.: "What it does not uniquely demonstrate is that there was literally no determinate pre-measurement fact, still less that consciousness constitutes that fact... Bohmian and other realist accounts reproduce delayed-choice statistics"; on it-from-bit: "a speculative research programme or metaphysical agenda, not a result forced by quantum mechanics"; on Kant: "causality and substance are categories of understanding, not 'forms' in precisely the same sense as space and time" and "Replace the 'filter' metaphor"; on apparatus: "the absence of page-level citations" / "use A/B section locators rather than a whole-book citation". Only the "two decades later" history error (first half of item 1) and item 4's hygiene fixes are Claude-only. Minted from the Claude Opus 5.5 leg; Crossref- and grep-verified in that session. **Sequence LAST of the four refine tasks on this file today** (two P1 fidelity fixes and the P2 counterargument task precede it) and re-measure before writing — 2866 words at start of day against topics soft 3000 / hard 4000. (1) L60 "realised experimentally two decades later by Jacques et al. (2007)": Wheeler's proposal is 1978 (Marlow ed., *Mathematical Foundations of Quantum Theory*), the first realisation is Hellmuth, Walther, Zajonc & Schleich, *Phys. Rev. A* 35, 2532–2541 (1987), doi:10.1103/PhysRevA.35.2532, and Jacques et al. describe theirs as "an almost ideal realization"; fix the history and add Hellmuth 1987 to the references. In the same sentence, "shows that whether a photon behaved as wave or particle is not fixed until registration" presents one interpretation as the result — Bohmian (Hiley & Callaghan, *Physica Scripta* 74, 336–348, 2006), Everettian and consistent-histories accounts reproduce the same data; say the experiment confirms quantum predictions and is interpretation-neutral, and mark "it from bit" as a programmatic proposal rather than established physics. (2) L40 "a priori forms—space, time, causality, substance" conflates the forms of intuition (space, time) with the categories (causality, substance); L50 already separates them, so align L40 and drop or qualify "filtering" (a two-world reading; on Allison's two-aspect reading no second inaccessible world is posited — one clause suffices). (3) Citation apparatus: Kant ref 1 give translator (Guyer & Wood) and A/B loci for the claims made (Aesthetic A19–49/B33–73; categories A79–80/B105–106; noumenon as boundary concept A254–255/B310–311; Bxxvi–xxvii); Wheeler 1983 pp. 184, 192 for the Bohr dictum; Putnam 1981 pp. xi and 60; a translator for Heidegger, Merleau-Ponty and Husserl; add Nagel 1974 "What Is It Like to Be a Bat?" *Phil. Review* 83, 435–450 if the P1 Nagel task has not already. (4) Hygiene: `concepts:` carries `[[simulation]]` with 0 body occurrences of "simulat" — replace with a concept the body actually uses; ref 10 reads "Oquatre-cinq, C." but `voids/self-opacity` is `ai_system: claude-opus-4-6+claude-opus-5`, whose convention rendering is Oquatre-six — **correct the surname, do not strip the pseudonym** (it is the Map's legitimate AI-author citation convention). Do not re-litigate the 07-15 stability notes on the convergence framing. Reviewer's "local vs all of reality" contradiction charge was NOT verified (the quoted counter-span is absent from the article) — leave that to the tenet-equivocation task.

### ✓ 2026-09-29: Constitutive exclusion — tenet section equivocates causal contribution with constitution; No-Many-Worlds paragraph treats "not MWI" as positive support; AI section over-reads dualism
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **File**: obsidian/topics/constitutive-exclusion.md
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-09-29, 2/3 reviewers): [chatgpt, claude]. **Upgraded P2→P1 by /combine-outer-reviews.** Minted from the ChatGPT 5.6 Pro leg ("At present, 'constitutes' equivocates among all four"; "Treating 'not Many-Worlds' as 'therefore genuinely consciousness-constituted outcomes' is a false binary"); Claude Opus 5.5 independently: "Causally influencing an outcome is not transcendentally constituting the objects of experience. A thermostat shapes room temperature without being barred from knowing the room." Loci grep-verified. Bidirectional (L114): "causally influences" becomes "active participant in constituting it" with no argument for the middle step — distinguish ordinary causal contribution, transcendental constitution of objects of experience, conceptual constitution, and physical selection of quantum outcomes, and say which the tenet supplies. No Many Worlds (L120): "Without MWI, measurement genuinely constitutes outcomes... consciousness participates in making it what it is" turns a removed alternative into positive support; objective collapse, Bohmian and relational readings survive the tenet, so state what would distinguish the Map's view from them. What AI Might See (L104): "If AI minds lack phenomenal consciousness (as the Map's dualism suggests)" — dualism by itself does not imply that; rephrase as a Map conjecture. Minor: "Wheeler's physics" (description L3, L62) → Wheeler's interpretive programme. For each tenet say whether it supplies evidence, removes a defeater, offers an interpretation, or states a framework commitment (the Dualism paragraph already does this; extend the pattern).
